\varpi^{(l)}_{{\rm crys}}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}):=\varpi^{(l,0)}_{{\rm crys}}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T})
.
Set also

\varpi^{(m)}_{{\rm zar}}(\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}):=\varpi^{(0,m)}_{{\rm zar}}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}_{0})

and
\varpi^{(m)}_{{\rm crys}}(\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}):=\varpi^{(0,m)}_{{\rm crys}}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T})
.

Definition 4.3
.

We call

\displaystyle\varpi^{(l,m)}_{\rm zar}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}_{0})\quad{\rm and}\quad\varpi^{(l,m)}_{\rm crys}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T})

the
zariskian orientation sheaf
of

\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}

and the
crystalline orientation sheaf
of

\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}}/(\overset{\circ}{T},{\cal J},\delta)
, respectively.
We also call
\varpi^{(l)}_{{\rm zar}}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T})
and

\varpi^{(l)}_{{\rm crys}}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T})

the
zariskian orientation sheaf
of
\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}

and the
crystalline orientation sheaf
of
\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}
.
We also call
\varpi^{(m)}_{{\rm zar}}(\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T})
and

\varpi^{(m)}_{{\rm crys}}(\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T})

the
zariskian orientation sheaf
of
\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}

and the
crystalline orientation sheaf
of
\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}
.

Lemma 4.4
.

(1)
Let
\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{(l,m)}
and
{\mathfrak{D}}

be the
(
log
)
PD-envelopes of

\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(l)}\cap\overset{\circ}{\cal D}{}^{(m)}
over

(\overset{\circ}{T},{\cal J},\delta)

and
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}{\cal X}
over
(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)
,
respectively.
Then
\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{(l,m)}=\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}\times_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}}(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(l)}\cap\overset{\circ}{\cal D}{}^{(m)})
.

(2)
Let
{\mathfrak{D}}({\cal D}^{(k)})
be the log PD-envelope of

D^{(k)}_{T_{0}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}{\cal D}^{(k)}
over
(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)
.
Then
{\mathfrak{D}}({\cal D})^{(k)}={\mathfrak{D}}\times_{{\cal X}}{\cal D}^{(k)}
.

Proof.

(1), (2):
Because we have natural morphisms

\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{(l,m)}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}
and

{\mathfrak{D}}({\cal D}^{(k)})\longrightarrow{\mathfrak{D}}
, the questions are local on
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
.
Hence we may assume that
M_{S(T)^{\natural}}
is free of rank
1
and that
there exists the following cartesian diagram

(4.4.1)

\begin{CD}(X,D)@>{\subset}>{}>({\cal X},{\cal D})\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
{\mathbb{A}}_{S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}(a,b,d,e)@>{\subset}>{}>{\mathbb{A}}_{S(T)^{\natural}}(a,b,d^{\prime},e)\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}@>{\subset}>{}>S(T)^{\natural},\end{CD}

where
d^{\prime}\geq d
and the vertical morphism

({\cal X},{\cal D})\longrightarrow{\mathbb{A}}_{S(T)^{\natural}}(a,b,d^{\prime},e)
is solid and étale and
the immersion
{\mathbb{A}}_{S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}(a,b,d,e)\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}{\mathbb{A}}_{S(T)^{\natural}}(a,b,d^{\prime},e)
is defined by
the “extra coordinates”
x_{d+1},\ldots,x_{d^{\prime}}
of
{\mathbb{A}}_{S(T)^{\natural}}(a,b,d^{\prime},e)
.
Now (1) and (2) follow from the local descriptions of

\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{(l,m)}
,
{\mathfrak{D}}
and

{\mathfrak{D}}({\cal D}^{(k)})
.
∎

The following is a generalization of
[
N4
, (1.3.14)]
:

Proposition 4.5
.

Identify the points of
\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}

with those of
\overset{\circ}{X}
.
Then the following hold
:

(1)
Let
r
be a nonnegative integer such that

M_{(X,D),z}/{\cal O}_{X,z}^{*}\simeq{\mathbb{N}}^{r}
.
Let
x_{\lambda_{l}}
and
y_{\mu_{m}}
be
the corresponding local coordinates to

{\cal X}_{\lambda_{l}}\not=\emptyset
and

{\cal D}_{\mu_{m}}\not=\emptyset
, respectively, around
z
.
Let

b^{(l,m)}_{\mathfrak{D}}\colon\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{(l,m)}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}\quad(l,m\in{\mathbb{N}})

be the natural morphism.
Then, for a positive integer
k
,
the following morphism

(4.5.1)

\displaystyle{\rm Res}\colon P_{k}({\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})\longrightarrow

\displaystyle{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}\bigoplus_{l+m=k}b^{(l-1),(m)}_{*}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(l-1)}\cap\overset{\circ}{\cal D}{}^{(m)}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(l-1),(m)}_{\rm zar}((\overset{\circ}{\cal X}+\overset{\circ}{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}))[-k]

\displaystyle\sigma\otimes d\log x_{\lambda_{0}}\wedge\cdots\wedge d\log x_{\lambda_{l-1}}\wedge d\log y_{\mu_{0}}\wedge\cdots\wedge d\log y_{\mu_{m-1}}\wedge\omega\longmapsto

\displaystyle\sigma\otimes b^{*}_{\lambda_{0},\cdots,\lambda_{l-1},\mu_{0}\cdots\mu_{m-1}}(\omega)\otimes({\rm orientation}~(\lambda_{0}\cdots\lambda_{l-1})\otimes(\mu_{0}\cdots\mu_{m-1}))

(\sigma\in{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}},\omega\in P_{0}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\cal X}/\overset{\circ}{T}})

(cf.
[
D1
, (3.1.5)]
)

around
z

induces the following “Poincaré residue isomorphism”
:

(4.5.2)

\displaystyle{\rm Res}\colon{\rm gr}_{k}^{P}({\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{l+m=k}b^{(l-1),(m)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}*}({\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{(l-1),(m)}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(l-1)}\cap\overset{\circ}{\cal D}{}^{(m)}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(l-1)}\cap\overset{\circ}{\cal D}{}^{(m)}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(l-1),(m)}_{\rm zar}((\overset{\circ}{\cal X}+\overset{\circ}{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}))[-k].

(2)

Let
z
be a point of
\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}
.
Let
r
be a nonnegative integer such that

M(D)_{X,z}/{\cal O}_{X,z}^{*}\simeq{\mathbb{N}}^{r}
.
Let
y_{\mu_{m}}
be the corresponding local coordinate to

{\cal X}_{\lambda_{m}}\not=\emptyset
around
z
.
Let

d^{(k)}_{\mathfrak{D}}({\cal D})\colon{\mathfrak{D}}^{(k)}({\cal D})\longrightarrow{\mathfrak{D}}\quad(k\in{\mathbb{N}})

be the natural morphism.
Then, for a positive integer
k
,
the following morphism

(4.5.3)

{\rm Res}^{\cal D}\colon P^{\cal D}_{k}({\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})\longrightarrow{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}d^{(k)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}}({\cal D})_{*}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{{\mathfrak{D}}^{(k)}({\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal D}/\overset{\circ}{T}))[-k]

\sigma\otimes d\log y_{\mu_{0}}\wedge\cdots\wedge d\log y_{\mu_{k-1}}\wedge\omega\longmapsto\sigma\otimes c^{*}_{\mu_{0}\cdots\mu_{k-1}}(\omega)\otimes({\rm orientation}~(\mu_{0}\cdots\mu_{k-1}))

(\sigma\in{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}},\omega\in P^{\cal D}_{0}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})

(cf.
[
D1
, (3.1.5)]
)

around
z

induces the following “Poincaré residue isomorphism”

(4.5.4)

\displaystyle{\rm gr}^{P^{\cal D}}_{k}({\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})

\displaystyle\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}d^{(k)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}}({\cal D})_{*}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}^{(k)}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal D}^{(k)}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{{\cal D}^{(k)}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal D}/\overset{\circ}{T}))[-k].

(3)

Let
z
be a point of
\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}
.
Let
r
be a nonnegative integer such that

M_{X,z}/{\cal O}_{X,z}^{*}\simeq{\mathbb{N}}^{r}
.
Let
x_{\lambda_{l}}
be the corresponding local coordinate to

{\cal D}_{\mu_{l}}\not=\emptyset
around
z
.
Let
{\mathfrak{D}}^{(k)}

(k\in{\mathbb{N}})
be the the log scheme
whose underlying scheme is
\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{(k),(0)}

and whose log structure is the pull-back of

(\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)},{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}})
.
Let

d^{(k)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}}({\cal X})_{*}\colon{\mathfrak{D}}^{(k)}\longrightarrow{\mathfrak{D}}\quad(k\in{\mathbb{N}})

be the natural morphism.
Then, for a positive integer
k
,
the following morphism

(4.5.5)

{\rm Res}^{\cal D}\colon P^{\cal X}_{k}({\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})\longrightarrow{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}d^{(k)}_{*}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)},\overset{\circ}{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}^{(k)}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal D}/\overset{\circ}{T}))[-k]

\sigma\otimes d\log y_{\mu_{0}}\wedge\cdots\wedge d\log y_{\mu_{k-1}}\wedge\omega\longmapsto\sigma\otimes c^{*}_{\mu_{0}\cdots\mu_{k-1}}(\omega)\otimes({\rm orientation}~(\mu_{0}\cdots\mu_{k-1}))

(\sigma\in{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}},\omega\in P^{\cal D}_{0}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})

(cf.
[
D1
, (3.1.5)]
)

around
z

induces the following “Poincaré residue isomorphism”

(4.5.6)

\displaystyle{\rm gr}^{P^{\cal X}}_{k}({\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})

\displaystyle\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}d^{(k)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}}({\cal X})_{*}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}^{(k)}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal D}^{(k)}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)},\overset{\circ}{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}^{(k)}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal D}/\overset{\circ}{T}))[-k].

Proof.

Because the proofs of (1) and (3) are similar to that of (2),
we give only the proof of (2).
As in the usual case, we can easily check that
the morphism (
4.5.3
) is well-defined and surjective.
Because the question is local on
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
,
we may assume that
M_{S(T)^{\natural}}
is free of rank
1

and that there exists the cartesian diagram (
4.4.1
).
Then
we have the following isomorphism

(4.5.7)

{\rm Res}\colon{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{\cal D}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}}\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}d^{(k)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}*}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{{\cal D}{}^{(k)}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal P}/\overset{\circ}{T})[-k]\quad(k\geq 1)

(cf. the Poincaré residue isomorphism
[
NS
, (2.2.21.3)]
).
By (
4.1
)
we have the following exact sequence

(4.5.8)

\displaystyle 0\longrightarrow

\displaystyle P^{\cal D}_{k-1}({\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})\longrightarrow P^{\cal D}_{k}({\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}d^{(k)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}*}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{{\cal D}{}^{(k)}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal D}/\overset{\circ}{T}))[-k]\longrightarrow 0.

This is nothing but (
4.5.4
) by (
4.4
) (2).
∎

Proposition 4.6

(
cf.
[
M
, Lemma 3.15.1]
,
[
N1
, (6.29)]
,
[
N4
, (1.3.21)]
)
.

Let

e^{(k)}\colon(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)},\overset{\circ}{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)}})\longrightarrow(\overset{\circ}{{\cal X}},\overset{\circ}{\cal D})

(k\in{\mathbb{N}})

be the natural morphism.
Set
{\mathfrak{D}}^{(k)}:={\mathfrak{D}}\times_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}}\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)}

(k\in{\mathbb{N}})
.
Fix a total order on
\Lambda
once and for all.
For an element
\underline{\lambda}=\{\lambda_{0},\ldots,\lambda_{k}\}

(\lambda_{i}<\lambda_{j}~{\rm if}~i<j,\lambda_{i}\in\Lambda)
,
set
\underline{\lambda}_{j}:=\underline{\lambda}\setminus\{\lambda_{j}\}

and let
\iota_{\underline{\lambda}_{j},\underline{\lambda}}\colon{\cal X}_{\underline{\lambda}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}{\cal X}_{\underline{\lambda}_{j}}

be the natural inclusion.
Let
{\mathfrak{D}}_{\underline{\lambda}}
be the log PD-envelope of the immersion

X_{\underline{\lambda}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}{\cal X}_{\underline{\lambda}}
over
(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)

and let
b_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\underline{\lambda}}}\colon{\mathfrak{D}}_{\underline{\lambda}}\longrightarrow{\mathfrak{D}}

be the natural morphism.
Let

\displaystyle\iota^{*}_{\underline{\lambda}_{j},\underline{\lambda}}\colon

\displaystyle b_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\underline{\lambda}_{j}}*}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\underline{\lambda}_{j}}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal X}_{\underline{\lambda}_{j}}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{({\cal X}_{\underline{\lambda}_{j}},\overset{\circ}{\cal D}|_{{\cal X}_{\underline{\lambda}_{j}}})/\overset{\circ}{T}})\longrightarrow b_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\underline{\lambda}}*}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\underline{\lambda}}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal X}_{\underline{\lambda}}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{({\cal X}_{\underline{\lambda}},\overset{\circ}{\cal D}|_{{\cal X}_{\underline{\lambda}}})/\overset{\circ}{T}})

be the induced morphism by
\iota_{\underline{\lambda}_{j},\underline{\lambda}}
.
Set

(4.6.1)

\displaystyle\iota^{(k)*}:=\underset{\{\underline{\lambda}|\#\underline{\lambda}=k+1\}}{\sum}\sum_{j=0}^{k}(-1)^{j}\iota^{*}_{\underline{\lambda}_{j},\underline{\lambda}}\colon

\displaystyle b^{(k-1)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}*}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}^{(k-1)}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k-1)}}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k-1)},\overset{\circ}{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k-1)}})/\overset{\circ}{T}})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow b^{(k)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}*}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}^{(k)}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)}}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)},\overset{\circ}{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}).

Then the following sequence

(4.6.2)

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\longrightarrow P^{\cal X}_{0}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})\longrightarrow b^{(0)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}*}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}^{(0)}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(0)}}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(0)},\overset{\circ}{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(0)}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(0)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}/\overset{\circ}{T}))

\displaystyle\overset{\iota^{(0)*}}{\longrightarrow}{b}{}^{(1)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}*}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}^{(1)}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(1)}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(1)},\overset{\circ}{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(1)}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(1)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}/\overset{\circ}{T}))\overset{\iota^{(1)*}}{\longrightarrow}\cdots

is exact.

Proof.

(The proof is the same as that of
[
N4
, (1.3.21)]
.)
Because the question is local on
\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}
,
we may assume that
M_{S(T)^{\natural}}
is free of rank
1

and that there exists the following cartesian diagram

(4.6.3)

\begin{CD}(X,D)@>{\subset}>{}>(\overline{\cal X},\overline{\cal D})\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
{\mathbb{A}}_{S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}(a,b,d,e)@>{\subset}>{}>{\mathbb{A}}_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}(a,b,d^{\prime},e)\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}@>{\subset}>{}>\overline{S(T)^{\natural}},\end{CD}

where
d^{\prime}\geq d
and the vertical morphism

({\cal X},{\cal D})\longrightarrow{\mathbb{A}}_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}(a,b,d^{\prime},e)
is solid and étale and
the immersion
{\mathbb{A}}_{S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}(a,b,d,e)\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}{\mathbb{A}}_{S(T)^{\natural}}(a,b,d^{\prime},e)
is defined by
the extra coordinates
x_{d+1},\ldots,x_{d^{\prime}}
of
{\mathbb{A}}_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}(a,b,d^{\prime})
.
Because the morphisms

P_{0}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}\Omega^{i}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})\longrightarrow b^{(0)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}*}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}^{(0)}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(0)}}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(0)},\overset{\circ}{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(0)}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(0)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}/\overset{\circ}{T}))

and
\iota^{(k)*}
are
{\cal O}_{\cal X}
-linear, we may assume that

\overline{\cal P}:=(\overline{\cal X},\overline{\cal D})={\mathbb{A}}_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}(a,b,d^{\prime},e)
.
Set
{\cal P}:=\overline{\cal P}\times_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}S(T)^{\natural}
,

\overline{\cal Q}:={\mathbb{A}}_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}(a,d-b)
,

{\cal Q}:=\overline{\cal Q}\times_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}S(T)^{\natural}
and

{\cal R}:=({\mathbb{A}}^{b}_{\overset{\circ}{T}},(y_{1}\cdots y_{b}=0))
.
Let

b^{\prime}{}^{(k)}\colon\overset{\circ}{\cal Q}{}^{(k)}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{\cal Q}

be the natural morphism.
By
[
DI
, (4.2.2) (c)]
we have the following exact sequence

0\longrightarrow\Omega^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{\overline{\cal Q}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}/{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\overline{\cal Q}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}(-\overset{\circ}{\cal Q})\longrightarrow b^{(0)}_{*}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{\cal Q}{}^{(0)}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(0)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal Q}/\overset{\circ}{T}))\longrightarrow\cdots.

Because

\displaystyle{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}}}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\overline{\cal X}},\overset{\circ}{\overline{\cal D}}|_{\overset{\circ}{\overline{\cal P}}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}/{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\overline{\cal P}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}(-\overset{\circ}{\cal P}))\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle(\Omega^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{\overline{\cal Q}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}/\Omega^{\bullet}_{\overline{\cal Q}/\overset{\circ}{T}}(-\overset{\circ}{\cal Q})\otimes_{{\cal O}_{T}}

\displaystyle{\cal O}_{T}\langle x_{d+1},\ldots,x_{d^{\prime}}\rangle\otimes_{{\cal O}_{T}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{{\cal R}/\overset{\circ}{T}},

because

\displaystyle{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal P}}b{}^{(k)}_{*}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)},{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal P}/\overset{\circ}{T}))\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle b^{\prime}{}^{(k)}_{*}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{\cal Q}{}^{(k)}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal Q}/\overset{\circ}{T}))\otimes_{{\cal O}_{T}}

\displaystyle{\cal O}_{T}\langle x_{d+1},\ldots,x_{d^{\prime}}\rangle\otimes_{{\cal O}_{T}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{{\cal R}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

and because the complex

{\cal O}_{T}\langle x_{d+1},\ldots,x_{d}\rangle\otimes_{{\cal O}_{T}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{{\cal R}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

consists of free
{\cal O}_{T}
-modules,
we see that the following sequence is exact:

(4.6.4)

0\longrightarrow{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}}}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\overline{\cal X}},\overline{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\overline{\cal X}}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}/{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\overline{\cal P}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}(-\overset{\circ}{\cal P}))\longrightarrow{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal P}}b^{(0)}_{*}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(0)},\overline{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\overline{\cal X}}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(0)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal P}/\overset{\circ}{T}))\longrightarrow\cdots.

By (
4.2
) (4) and (
4.2.1
) we have
the following isomorphism:

(4.6.5)

\displaystyle\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\overline{\cal X}},\overline{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\overline{\cal X}}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}/\Omega^{\bullet}_{{\overline{\cal P}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}(-\overset{\circ}{\cal P})\simeq P_{0}^{\cal X}\Omega^{\bullet}_{\overline{\cal P}/\overset{\circ}{T}}/\Omega^{\bullet}_{{\overline{\cal P}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}(-\overset{\circ}{\cal P})=P_{0}^{\cal X}\Omega^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}/\overset{\circ}{T}}.

By (
4.4
) (1), (
4.6.4
) and
(
4.6.5
), we see that the sequence (
4.6.2
) is exact.
∎

Lemma 4.7
.

Let
k
be a positive integer.
Set
\theta:=\Omega^{1}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})
be the image of

d\log t\in\Omega^{1}_{S(T)^{\natural}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
, where
t
is the local section of
M_{S(T)^{\natural}}

whose image in
M_{S(T)^{\natural}}/{\cal O}_{T}^{*}
is the generator.

(
the local section
d\log t
is independent of the choice of
t.)

Then the following diagram is commutative
:

(4.7.1)

\begin{CD}{\rm gr}^{P^{\cal X}}_{k+1}({\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal X}}}{\Omega}^{i+1}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})@>{\simeq}>{}>\\
@A{\theta\wedge}A{}A\\
{\rm gr}^{P^{\cal X}}_{k}({\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}\Omega^{i}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}})@>{\simeq}>{}>\end{CD}

\begin{CD}{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}}}\Omega^{i-k-1}_{(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)},{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{k})})/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}/\overset{\circ}{T})))\\
@A{}A{\iota^{(k-1)*}}A\\
{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}}}\Omega^{i-k}_{(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{{\rm ex},(k-1)},{\cal D}|_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{{\rm ex},(k-1)}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k-1)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}/\overset{\circ}{T})).\end{CD}

Proof.

The proof is the same as that of
[
M
, 4.12]
(cf.
[
N1
, (10.1.16)]
).
∎

5
Zariskian
p
-adic El Zein-Steenbrink-Zucker bifiltered complexes

Let
S
,
(T,{\cal J},\delta)
,
T_{0}\longrightarrow S
,
S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
and
S(T)^{\natural}

be as in previous sections.
Let
(X,D)/S
be an SNCL scheme with a relative SNCD on
X/S
.
Let

f\colon(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\longrightarrow S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}

be the structural morphism.
By abuse of notation, let us also denote the structural morphism

X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\longrightarrow S(T)^{\natural}
by
f
.
Let
E
be a flat quasi-coherent crystal of

{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
-modules.

The aim in this section is to construct a bifiltered complex

(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)\in{\rm D}^{+}{\rm F}^{2}(f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T})),

which we call
the
zariskian
p
-adic bifiltered El Zein-Steenbrink-Zucker complex

of
E
for
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}
.

For the time being, assume that
there exists an immersion
(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}
into a log smooth scheme over
\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}
.
Denote
\overline{\cal P}{}^{\rm ex}=(\overline{\cal X},\overline{\cal D})
, where

(\overline{\cal X},\overline{\cal D})
is a strictly semistable formal scheme
with a relative SNCD over
\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}
.
Set
{\cal P}^{\rm ex}:=\overline{\cal P}{}^{\rm ex}\times_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}S(T)^{\natural}

and
({\cal X},{\cal D}):=(\overline{\cal X},\overline{\cal D})\times_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}S(T)^{\natural}(={\cal P}^{\rm ex})
.
Let
\overline{\mathfrak{D}}
be the log PD-envelope of
the immersion
(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}
over

(\overset{\circ}{T},{\cal J},\delta)
.
Set
{\mathfrak{D}}:=\overline{\mathfrak{D}}\times_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}S(T)^{\natural}
.
Let
(\overline{\cal E},\overline{\nabla})
be the quasi-coherent
{\cal O}_{\overline{\mathfrak{D}}}
-module
with the integrable connection
associated to
\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}(E)
:

\overline{\nabla}\colon\overline{\cal E}\longrightarrow\overline{\cal E}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\overline{\cal P}}}\Omega^{1}_{\overline{\cal P}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
.
Set
{\cal E}:=\overline{\cal E}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\overline{\mathfrak{D}}}}{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}
.
It is easy to check that
\overline{\nabla}

induces the following integrable connection

(5.0.1)

\nabla\colon{\cal E}\longrightarrow{\cal E}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}}}\Omega^{1}_{{\cal P}/\overset{\circ}{T}}={\cal E}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}}}{\Omega}^{1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}/\overset{\circ}{T}}.

Set

(5.0.2)

\displaystyle A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E})^{ij}

\displaystyle:={\cal E}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}/\overset{\circ}{T}}/P_{j}^{\cal X}

\displaystyle:={\cal E}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal X}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}/\overset{\circ}{T}}/P^{\cal X}_{j}({\cal E}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\cal X}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}/\overset{\circ}{T}})\quad(i,j\in{\mathbb{N}}).

The sheaf

A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E})^{ij}

has quotient filtrations
P^{\cal D}
and
P
obtained by
the filtrations
P^{\cal D}
and
P
on

{\cal E}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal X}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{({\cal X},{\cal D})/\overset{\circ}{T}}
.
We consider the following boundary morphisms of
double complexes:

(5.0.3)

\begin{CD}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E})^{i,j+1}\\
@A{\theta\wedge}A{}A\\
A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E})^{ij}@>{-\nabla}>{}>A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E})^{i+1,j}.\\
\end{CD}

(We think that these are the best boundary morphisms with respect to
the signs.)
Then we have the double complex

A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E})^{\bullet\bullet}
.
The complex

A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E})^{\bullet\bullet}

has filtrations
P^{\cal D}=\{P^{\cal D}_{k}\}_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}

and
P=\{P_{k}\}_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}

defined by the following formulas:

(5.0.4)

P^{\cal D}_{k}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E})^{\bullet\bullet}:=(\cdots P^{\cal D}_{k}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E})^{ij}\cdots)\in C^{+}(f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T}))

and

(5.0.5)

P_{k}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E})^{\bullet\bullet}:=(\cdots P_{2j+k+1}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E})^{ij}\cdots)\in C^{+}(f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T})).

Let
(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}),P^{\cal D},P)

be the bifiltered single complex of the bifiltered double complex

(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E})^{\bullet\bullet},P^{\cal D},P)
.

Let
(Y,E)
be an SNCL scheme over
S
with a relative SNCD on
Y/S
and
assume that there exists an immersion

(Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},E_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal Q}

into a log smooth scheme over
\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}
.
Assume that there exist morphisms

g\colon(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\longrightarrow(Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},E_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})
and

\overline{g}\colon\overline{\cal P}\longrightarrow\overline{\cal Q}

making the following diagram commutative:

\begin{CD}(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})@>{\subset}>{}>\overline{\cal P}\\
@V{g}V{}V@V{}V{\overline{g}}V\\
(Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},E_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})@>{\subset}>{}>\overline{\cal Q}.\end{CD}

Set
{\cal Q}:=\overline{\cal Q}\times_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}S(T)^{\natural}
.
Let
{\mathfrak{D}}
and
{\mathfrak{E}}
be the log PD-envelopes of
the immersion
(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}{\cal P}

and
(Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},E_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}{\cal Q}

over
(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)
, respectively.
Let
\widetilde{g}\colon{\cal P}\longrightarrow{\cal Q}
be
the base change morphism of
\overline{g}

by the morphism

S(T)^{\natural}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}
.
Let

g^{\rm PD}\colon{\mathfrak{D}}\longrightarrow{\mathfrak{E}}

be the natural morphism induced by
\widetilde{g}
.
Let

\overset{\circ}{g}_{\rm crys}\colon((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}})\longrightarrow((\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}})

be the induced morphism of ringed topoi by

\overset{\circ}{g}\colon\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T_{0}}
.
Let
E
(resp.
F
) be a flat quasi-coherent crystal of

{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
-modules
(resp. a flat quasi-coherent crystal of

{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
-modules).
Assume that we are given
a morphism
F\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{g}_{{\rm crys}*}(E)
.
Let
({\cal F},\nabla)
be
the
{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}
-module
with integrable connection obtained in (
5.0.1
) for
F
.
By (
3.9
)

{\cal P}^{\rm ex}
and
{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}
are
SNCL schemes
({\cal X},{\cal D)}
and

({\cal Y},{\cal E)}
over
S(T)^{\natural}
with SNCD on
{\cal X}
and
{\cal Y}
,
respectively, and the morphism

{\cal P}\longrightarrow{\cal Q}
is equal to
({\cal X},{\cal D})\longrightarrow({\cal Y},{\cal E})
over

(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)
.
Then we have the following morphism of trifiltered complexes:

(5.0.6)

({\cal F}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}/\overset{\circ}{T}},P^{\cal E},P,P^{\cal Y})\longrightarrow g^{\rm PD}_{*}(({\cal E}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}/\overset{\circ}{T}},P^{\cal D},P,P^{\cal X})).

Hence we have the following morphism of bifiltered complexes:

(5.0.7)

(A_{\rm zar}({\cal Q}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal F}),P^{\cal E},P)\longrightarrow g^{\rm PD}_{*}((A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}),P^{\cal D},P)).

Now we come back to the situation in the beginning of this section.

Let
(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})
be
the disjoint union of affine open coverings of

(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})
.
Let
(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime}

be an immersion into a log smooth scheme over
\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}

(this immersion exists if
each log affine subscheme of

X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
is sufficiently small).
Set
X_{{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}n}:={\rm cosk}_{0}^{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})_{n}

and

\overline{\cal P}_{n}:={\rm cosk}_{0}^{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}(\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime})_{n}

(n\in{\mathbb{N}})
.
Then we have a natural immersion

(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet}

over
S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}
.
Let
\overline{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}

be the log PD-envelope of the immersion

X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet}

over
(\overset{\circ}{T},{\cal J},\gamma)
.
Set
{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}:=\overline{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}\times_{{\mathfrak{D}}(\overline{S(T)^{\natural}})}S(T)^{\natural}
.
Let
f_{\bullet}\colon X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}\longrightarrow S(T)^{\natural}

be the structural morphism.
Set
{\cal P}_{\bullet}:=\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet}\times_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}S(T)^{\natural}
.
We have a natural immersion
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}{\cal P}_{\bullet}

over
S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}S(T)^{\natural}
.
Let
E^{\bullet}
be the flat quasi-coherent crystal
of
{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
-modules obtained by
E
.
Let
(\overline{\cal E}{}^{\bullet},\overline{\nabla}{}^{\bullet})

be the quasi-coherent
{\cal O}_{\overline{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}}
-module
with the integrable connection
associated to
\epsilon^{*}_{X_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}(E^{\bullet})

\overline{\nabla}{}^{\bullet}\colon\overline{\cal E}{}^{\bullet}\longrightarrow\overline{\cal E}{}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet}}}\Omega^{1}_{\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
.
Set
{\cal E}^{\bullet}:={\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\overline{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}}}\overline{\cal E}{}^{\bullet}
.
The connection
\overline{\nabla}{}^{\bullet}

induces the following integrable connection

(5.0.8)

\nabla^{\bullet}\colon{\cal E}^{\bullet}\longrightarrow{\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{1}_{{\cal P}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}={\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}.

By (
3.9
)

is equal to a simplicial SNCL scheme

({\cal X}_{\bullet},{\cal D}_{\bullet})
with a simplicial SNCD over
S(T)^{\natural}
.
Hence we have the following cosimplicial trifiltered complex by (
5.0.6
):

(5.0.9)

({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}},P^{\cal D},P,P^{\cal X}).

Set

(5.0.10)

A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})^{ij}:=({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}})/P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{j}\quad(i,j\in{\mathbb{N}}).

We consider the following boundary morphisms of
the following double complex:

(5.0.11)

\begin{CD}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})^{i,j+1}\\
@A{\theta\wedge}A{}A\\
A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})^{ij}@>{-\nabla}>{}>A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})^{i+1,j}.\\
\end{CD}

Then we have the cosimplicial double complex

A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})^{\bullet\bullet}
.
The double complex

A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})^{\bullet\bullet}

has filtrations
P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}}=\{P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}}_{k}\}_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}
and

(5.0.12)

P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}}_{k}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}):=(\cdots P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}}_{k}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})^{ij}\cdots).

and

(5.0.13)

P_{k}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}):=(\cdots(P_{2j+k+1}+P_{j}^{\cal X})A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})^{ij}\cdots).

Let
(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}),P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}},P)

be the single bifiltered complex of the bifiltered double complex

(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})^{\bullet\bullet},P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}},P)
.

Let

(5.0.14)

a^{(l,m)}\colon\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}\quad(l,m\in{\mathbb{N}})

and

(5.0.15)

a^{(l,m)}_{\bullet}\colon\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}\bullet}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}\bullet}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}\bullet}\quad(l,m\in{\mathbb{N}})

be the natural morphisms of
schemes and the natural morphism of simplicial schemes.
Let
\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{(l,m)}_{\bullet}
and
{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}

be the (log) PD-envelopes of

\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}\bullet}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}\bullet}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(l)}_{\bullet}\cap\overset{\circ}{\cal D}{}^{(m)}_{\bullet}
over

(\overset{\circ}{T},{\cal J},\delta)

and
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}{\cal X}
over
(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)
,
respectively.
Let

(5.0.16)

b^{(l,m)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}}\colon\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{(l,m)}_{\bullet}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}\quad(l,m\in{\mathbb{N}})

be the natural morphism.
Let

(5.0.17)

a^{(l,m)}_{{\rm crys}}\colon((\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}})\longrightarrow((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}})\quad(l,m\in{\mathbb{N}})

and

(5.0.18)

a^{(l,m)}_{\bullet,{\rm crys}}\colon((\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}\bullet}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}})\longrightarrow((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}})\quad(l,m\in{\mathbb{N}})

be the morphisms of ringed topoi
obtained by (
5.0.14
) and (
5.0.15
),
respectively.
Let

(5.0.19)

c^{(k)}\colon D^{(k)}_{T_{0}}\longrightarrow X_{T_{0}}\quad(k\in{\mathbb{N}})

and

(5.0.20)

c^{(k)}_{\bullet}\colon D^{(k)}_{T_{0}\bullet}\longrightarrow X_{T_{0}\bullet}\quad(k\in{\mathbb{N}})

be the natural morphisms of
log schemes and the natural morphism of simplicial log schemes,
respectively.
Let

(5.0.21)

d^{(k)}({\cal D}_{\bullet})_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}}\colon{\mathfrak{D}}^{(k)}_{\bullet}({\cal D}_{\bullet})\longrightarrow{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}\quad(k\in{\mathbb{N}})

be the natural morphisms of

(5.0.22)

c^{(l)}_{{\rm crys}}\colon((D^{(l)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{D^{(l)}_{T_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}})\longrightarrow((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}})\quad(l,m\in{\mathbb{N}})

and

(5.0.23)

c^{(l)}_{\bullet{\rm crys}}\colon((D^{(l)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{D^{(l)}_{T_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}})\longrightarrow((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}})\quad(l,m\in{\mathbb{N}})

be the morphisms of ringed topoi

) and (

),

The following is only a constant simplicial SNCL with a relative SNCD version of
[
N3
, (4.14)]
.

Lemma 5.1
.

Let
k
be nonnegative integers.
For the morphism

g\colon{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{n}=({\cal X}_{n},{\cal D}_{n})\longrightarrow{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{n^{\prime}}=({\cal X}_{n^{\prime}},{\cal D}_{n^{\prime}})

corresponding to a morphism
[n^{\prime}]\longrightarrow[n]
in
\Delta
,
there exists morphisms

\overset{\circ}{g}{}^{(k)}_{\cal X}\colon\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)}_{n}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)}_{n^{\prime}}

(k\in{\mathbb{N}})

and

g^{(k)}_{\cal D}\colon{\cal D}^{(k)}_{n}\longrightarrow{\cal D}{}^{(k)}_{n^{\prime}}

(k\in{\mathbb{N}})

over the morphism
\overset{\circ}{\cal X}_{n}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{\cal X}_{n^{\prime}}
and

{\cal X}_{n}\longrightarrow{\cal X}_{n^{\prime}}
, respectively.
Consequently
\{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(k)}_{n}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}
and

\{{\cal D}^{(k)}_{n}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}
give us the
(
log
)
simplicial formal schemes

\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(l)}_{\bullet}\cap\overset{\circ}{\cal D}{}^{(m)}_{\bullet}

(l,m\in{\mathbb{N}})

and
{\cal D}{}^{(k)}_{\bullet}
.

Proof.

This immediately follows from the proofs of
[
N3
, (4.14)]
and
[
N4
, (1.4.1)]
.
∎

Lemma 5.2
.

Let
k
be a nonnegative integer.
Then the following hold
:

(1)
There exists an isomorphism

(5.2.1)

\displaystyle({\rm gr}^{P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}}}_{k}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}),P^{\cal X})=d^{(k)}({\cal D}_{\bullet}\hookrightarrow{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet})_{*}((A_{\rm zar}({\cal D}^{(k)}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T})),P^{\cal X})[-k]

in
C^{+}(f^{-1}_{\bullet}({\cal O}_{T}))
.

(2)
There exists an isomorphism

(5.2.2)

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{k}^{P}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle\bigoplus^{k}_{k^{\prime}=-\infty}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{\bullet}\cap\overset{\circ}{\cal D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

\displaystyle\quad\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm zar}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}\bullet}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}\bullet})/\overset{\circ}{T}),\nabla)[-2j-k].

(3)
There exists an isomorphism

(5.2.3)

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P}{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{\overset{\circ}{D}}}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}}b^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k)}_{*}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{\bullet}\cap\overset{\circ}{\cal D}{}^{(k)}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

\displaystyle\quad\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k)}_{\rm et}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}\bullet}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}\bullet})/\overset{\circ}{T}))[-2j-k-k^{\prime}].

Proof.

(1), (2), (3):
These follow from (
5.1
) and (
4.5
).
∎

Let

(5.2.4)

\displaystyle\pi_{{\rm zar}}\colon

\displaystyle((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})_{\rm zar},f^{-1}_{\bullet}({\cal O}_{T}))\longrightarrow((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})_{\rm zar},f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T}))

be a natural morphism of ringed topoi.
Let

(5.2.5)

\displaystyle u_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}\colon((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}/S(T)^{\natural})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow(\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}})_{\rm zar},f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T}))

and

(5.2.6)

\displaystyle u_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\colon((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}})_{\rm zar},f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T}))

be the natural projections.
Let

(5.2.7)

\displaystyle\epsilon_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}\colon((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}})\longrightarrow((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}})

be the morphism forgetting the log structures of

(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})
and
S(T)^{\natural}
.

Proposition 5.3
.

There exists the following isomorphism

(5.3.1)

\displaystyle\theta:=\theta_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}\wedge\colon

\displaystyle Ru_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E)

\displaystyle\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}))

in
D^{+}(f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T}))
.
This isomorphism is independent of the choice of
an affine simplicial open covering of
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}

and the choice of a simplicial immersion

X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet}
over
\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}
.
In particular, the complex

R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}))

is independent of the choices above.

Proof.

First we claim that there exists an isomorphism from the source
to the target of (
5.3.1
).
Let

(5.3.2)

\displaystyle\pi_{{\rm crys}}\colon

\displaystyle((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})/S(T)^{\natural})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})/S(T)^{\natural}})\longrightarrow((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}})

be the natural morphism of ringed topoi.
Then, by the cohomological descent,
we have

\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E)=R\pi_{{\rm crys}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E^{\bullet}))
.
Let

(5.3.3)

\displaystyle u_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})/S(T)^{\natural}}\colon((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})/S(T)^{\natural})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})/S(T)^{\natural}})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})_{\rm zar},f^{-1}_{\bullet}({\cal O}_{T}))

be the natural projection.
Then
u_{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}}\circ\pi_{{\rm crys}}=\pi_{\rm zar}\circ u_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})/S(T)^{\natural}}
.
Hence
we have the following formula by the log Poincaré lemma:

(5.3.4)

\displaystyle Ru_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))

\displaystyle=Ru_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}R\pi_{{\rm crys}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E^{\bullet}))

\displaystyle=R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}Ru_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E^{\bullet}))

\displaystyle=R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet\,\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}).

Since
{\cal E}^{\bullet}
is a flat

{\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}}
-module,
it suffices to prove that the natural morphism

\displaystyle\theta\wedge

\displaystyle\colon{\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}\longrightarrow

\displaystyle\{({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}/P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{0}\overset{\theta}{\longrightarrow}{\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+2}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}/P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{1}\overset{\theta}{\longrightarrow}\cdots)\}

is a quasi-isomorphism.
As in
[
M
, 3.15]

(cf.
[
N1
, (6.28) (9), (6.29) (1)]
),
it suffices to prove that the sequence

(5.3.5)

\displaystyle 0\longrightarrow{\rm gr}_{0}^{P^{\cal X}_{\bullet}}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}}{\otimes}_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{n}}}{{\Omega}}^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}})\overset{\theta_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}\wedge}{\longrightarrow}{\rm gr}_{1}^{P^{\cal X}_{\bullet}}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{n}}{\otimes}_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{{\Omega}}^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}})[1]

\displaystyle\overset{\theta_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}\wedge}{\longrightarrow}{\rm gr}_{2}^{P^{\cal X}_{\bullet}}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{n}}{\otimes}_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}_{\bullet}^{\rm ex}}}{{\Omega}}^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}})[2]\overset{\theta_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}\wedge}{\longrightarrow}\cdots

is exact.
By (
4.7
) we have only to prove that
the following sequence

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\longrightarrow P^{{\cal X}_{n}}_{0}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{n}}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal X}_{n}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{({\cal X}_{n},{\cal D}_{n})/\overset{\circ}{T}})\longrightarrow b^{(0)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{n}({\cal X}_{n})*}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}^{(0)}_{n}({\cal X}_{n})}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(0)}_{\!n}}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(0)}_{\!n},\overset{\circ}{\cal D}_{n}|_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(0)}_{\!n}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(0)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}_{n}/\overset{\circ}{T}))

\displaystyle\overset{\iota^{(0)*}}{\longrightarrow}{b}{}^{(1)}_{{\mathfrak{D}}_{n}({\cal X}_{n})*}({\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{D}}^{(1)}_{n}({\cal X}_{n})}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(1)}_{\!n}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(1)}_{\!n},\overset{\circ}{\cal D}_{n}|_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(1)}_{\!n}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(1)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{\cal X}_{n}/\overset{\circ}{T}))\overset{\iota^{(1)*}}{\longrightarrow}\cdots

is exact. In (
4.6
) we have already proved this exactness.

Next we prove that the isomorphism is independent of
the choices in (
5.3
).

Let
(X^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})
be another disjoint union
of an affine simplicial open covering of

(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})
.
Set
(X^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}):=(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\times_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})}(X^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})
.
Then we have the simplicial log scheme

(X^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})
which is
the disjoint union of
the members of an (not necessarily affine) simplicial open covering
of
(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}

fitting into the following commutative diagram
:

(5.3.6)

\begin{CD}(X^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})@>{}>{}>(X^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})@>{}>{}>(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}).\end{CD}

Set
(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}n},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}n}):={\rm cosk}^{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})}_{0}((X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}))_{n}
and

(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}n},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}n}):={\rm cosk}^{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})}_{0}((X^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}))_{n}
.
Let

(X^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime}_{\bullet}
be another immersion in (
3.8.1
).
Then, by (
5.3.6
) and considering the fiber product

\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet}\times_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime}_{\bullet}
,
we may have the following commutative diagram

\begin{CD}(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})@>{\subset}>{}>\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet}\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})@>{\subset}>{}>\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime}_{\bullet}.\end{CD}

Set
{\cal P}^{\prime}_{\bullet}:=\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime}_{\bullet}\times_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}S(T)^{\natural}
.
Let
{\cal P}{}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\!\!\!\bullet}

be the exactification of
the immersion

X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}{\cal P}^{\prime}_{\bullet}
.
Let
{\cal E}^{\prime}{}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\prime\rm ex}_{\!\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\!\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

be an analogous complex
to

{\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
for
{\cal P}^{\prime}_{\bullet}
.
Then we have the following morphism

(5.3.7)

R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\!\!\!\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\prime}{}^{\bullet}))\longrightarrow R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})).

This morphism fits into the following commutative diagram

(5.3.8)

\begin{CD}R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\!\!\!\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\prime}{}^{\bullet}))@>{}>{}>R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}))\\
@A{R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(\theta_{{\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\!\!\!\bullet}}\wedge)}A{\simeq}A@A{\simeq}A{R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(\theta_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}\wedge)}A\\
R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}({\cal E}^{\prime}{}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\!\!\!\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\!\!\!\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}})=R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}).\end{CD}

This diagram tells us the desired independence
of the choices in (
5.3
).
∎

Next, by using
(
5.1
), (
5.2.3
) and (
5.3
),
we prove that the bifiltered complex

R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}((A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}),P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}},P))

depends only on
(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)
:

Theorem 5.4
.

The filtered complex

R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}((A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}),P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}},P))\in{\rm D}^{+}{\rm F}^{2}(f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T}))

is independent of the choice of
an affine simplicial open covering of

(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})

and the choice of a simplicial immersion

(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet}
over
\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}
.

Proof.

Let the notations be as in the proof of (
5.3
).
Then we have the following morphism

(5.4.1)

R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}((A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\!\!\!\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\prime}{}^{\bullet})),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)\longrightarrow R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}((A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})),P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}},P).

To prove that this is an isomorphism, it suffices to prove
that the morphism

(5.4.2)

R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}((P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}}_{k}\cap P_{l})A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\!\!\!\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\prime}{}^{\bullet}))\longrightarrow R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}((P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}}_{k}\cap P_{l})A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}))

is an isomorphism in
D^{+}(f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T}))
by (
5.3
).
Because the question that the morphism (
5.4.2
) is an isomorphism is
local on
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
,
we may assume that
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
is affine,
in particular, quasi-compact.
Hence we may assume that the filtrations
P^{D}
and
P
are biregular
and it suffices to prove
that the morphism

(5.4.3)

{\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P}{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{D}}R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\!\!\!\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\prime}{}^{\bullet}))\longrightarrow{\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P}{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{D}}R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}))

is an isomorphism in
D^{+}(f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T}))
.
By
[
NS
, (1.3.4.5)]
and (
5.2.3
) we have the following:

(5.4.4)

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P}{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{\overset{\circ}{D}}}R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}))\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}({\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P}{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{\overset{\circ}{D}}}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}))

\displaystyle\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}}b^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k)}_{*}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{\bullet}\cap\overset{\circ}{\cal D}{}^{(k)}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k)}_{\rm zar}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}\bullet}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}\bullet})/\overset{\circ}{T})))[-2j-k-k^{\prime}].

By the Poincaré lemma
the last complex is equal to

(5.4.5)

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(a^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}\bullet*}(Ru_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}\bullet}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(E|_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}\bullet}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi_{\rm crys}^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k)}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}\bullet}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}\bullet})/\overset{\circ}{T})))

\displaystyle[-2j-k-k^{\prime}]

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k,0\}}a^{(2j+k),(k^{\prime})}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}*}(Ru_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k)}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(E|_{{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}_{T_{0}}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k)}_{\rm crys}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T})))

\displaystyle[-2j-k^{\prime}-k]

and the analogous formula
for
R\pi_{m,{\rm zar}*}((A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\!\!\!m\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\prime}{}^{m\bullet}),P))
.

We complete the proof of (
5.4
).
∎

Definition 5.5
.

We call the bifiltered direct image

R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}((A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}),P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}}.P))

the
zariskian
p
-adic bifiltered El-Zein-Steenbrink-Zucker complex
of

E
for
(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)
.
We denote it by

(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)\in{\rm D}^{+}{\rm F}^{2}(f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T}))
.
When
E={\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
, we denote

(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)

by

(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)
.
We call

(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)

the
zariskian
p
-adic bifiltered El-Zein-Steenbrink-Zucker complex
of

(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)
.

Corollary 5.6
.

Let
E_{\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
be the inverse image of

E
to
({\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}/\overset{\circ}{T}})_{\rm crys}

and let

f_{D^{(k)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}}\colon(D^{(k)}/S(T)^{\natural})_{\rm crys}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{T}

be the structural morphism.
Then there exist the following spectral sequences
:

(5.6.1)

\displaystyle E_{1}^{k,q-k}

\displaystyle=R^{q-k}f_{D^{(k)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{D^{(k)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}}(E_{\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}/\overset{\circ}{T}})\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\epsilon^{-1}_{D^{(k)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm crys}((\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}_{0})))(-k)

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E)),

(5.6.2)

\displaystyle E_{1}^{-k,q+k}

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}R^{q-2j-k}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\cap D^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(E_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

\displaystyle\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime},k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm crys}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}))

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E)).

Proof.

(
5.6.1
) follows from the following:

(5.6.3)

\displaystyle R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}({\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{\overset{\circ}{D}}}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})){}\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}}b^{(k)}_{*}(\Omega^{\bullet}_{{\cal D}{}^{(k)}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T})))[-k]

\displaystyle\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}Ru_{D^{(k)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{D^{(k)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}(E_{\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}})\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\epsilon^{-1}_{D^{(k)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm crys}(\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}))[-k]

\displaystyle\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}Ru_{D^{(k)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}*}R\pi_{{\rm crys}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{D^{(k)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}(E_{\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}})\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\epsilon^{-1}_{D^{(k)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm crys}(\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}))[-k]

\displaystyle\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}Ru_{D^{(k)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{D^{(k)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}}(E_{\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}})\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\epsilon^{-1}_{D^{(k)}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm crys}(\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}))[-k].

(
5.6.2
) follows from the following:

\displaystyle R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}({\rm gr}_{k}^{P}A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})){}\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}

(5.6.4)

\displaystyle R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(\bigoplus_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{\cal X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{\bullet}\cap\overset{\circ}{\cal D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm zar}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}\bullet}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}\bullet})/\overset{\circ}{T}))[-2j-k].

\displaystyle\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}\bigoplus_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}a^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{\bullet*}Ru_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}\cap D^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(E_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

\displaystyle\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime},k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm crys}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}\bullet}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}\bullet})/\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}))[-2j-k]

\displaystyle\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}\bigoplus_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}a^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{*}Ru_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\cap D^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(E_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

\displaystyle\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime},k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm crys}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}_{0})))[-2j-k].

∎

Definition 5.7
.

We call the spectral sequences (
5.6.1
) and (
5.6.2
)
the
Poincaré spectral sequence
of

R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))

relative to

D

and the
Poincaré spectral sequence
of

R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))
, respectively.
We denote by
P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}
and
P
the filtrations on

R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))

obtained by (
5.6.1
) and (
5.6.2
), respectively.
If
E
is trivial, then
we call
P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}
and
P
the
weight filtration

on
R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))

relative to

D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
and the
weight filtration
on

R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))
,
respectively.

Proposition 5.8
.

The edge morphism

d^{-k,q+k}_{1}\colon E_{1,l}^{-k,q+k}\longrightarrow E_{1,l}^{-k+1,q+k}

of the spectral sequence
(

is
identified with the following morphism
:

(5.8.1)

\sum_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\sum_{j\geq{\rm max}\{-k^{\prime},0\}}\{-G^{(k),(k^{\prime})}+\iota^{(k),(k^{\prime})*}+(-1)^{2j+k^{\prime}+1}G^{(k),(k^{\prime})}(\overset{\circ}{D})\}.

Proof.

The proof is the same as that of
[
N1
, (10.1)]
.

Definition 6.2
.

e_{p}
the
exponent function
of
u

with respect to
p
.

Now we assume that

\overset{\circ}{T}
and
\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}
are
(
not necessarily affine
)

p
-adic formal schemes
such that
p\in{\cal J}
and
p\in{\cal J}^{\prime}

(
[
BO1
, 7.17, Definition]
).
Assume that
{\cal O}_{T}
is
p
-torsion-free.
Let

f_{\bullet}\colon{\cal P}_{\bullet}\longrightarrow S(T)^{\natural}

be the structural morphism.
Furthermore, for any
i,j\in{\mathbb{N}}
,
assume that the induced morphism

(6.2.1)

\displaystyle g^{{\rm PD}*}_{\bullet}\colon

\displaystyle{\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}}/P^{{\cal Y}_{\bullet}}_{j}\longrightarrow g^{\rm PD}_{\bullet*}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}}/P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{j})

by the following morphism

(6.2.2)

g^{{\rm PD}*}_{\bullet}\colon{\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}}\longrightarrow g^{\rm PD}_{\bullet*}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}})

is divisible by
p^{e_{p}(j+1)}
, that is,

g^{{\rm PD}*}_{\bullet}

at any point
x\in{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}

is divisible by
p^{e_{p}(f_{\bullet}(x))(j+1)}
.
Because the target of (
6.2.1
) is
{\cal O}_{T}
-flat,
the following morphism

(6.2.3)

\displaystyle{\rm deg}(u)^{-(j+1)}g^{{\rm PD}*}_{\bullet}\colon

\displaystyle{\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}}/P^{{\cal Y}_{\bullet}}_{j}\longrightarrow g^{\rm PD}_{\bullet*}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}}/P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{j})

for
j\in{\mathbb{N}}
is well-defined.
In fact, the following morphisms

(6.2.4)

\displaystyle{\rm deg}(u)^{-(j+1)}g^{{\rm PD}*}_{\bullet}\colon

\displaystyle(P^{{\cal C}_{\bullet}}_{k}+P^{{\cal Y}_{\bullet}}_{j})({\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}})/P^{{\cal Y}_{\bullet}}_{j}

\displaystyle\longrightarrow g^{\rm PD}_{\bullet*}((P^{{\cal D}_{\bullet}}_{k}+P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{j})({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}}/P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{j}))

and

(6.2.5)

\displaystyle{\rm deg}(u)^{-(j+1)}g^{{\rm PD}*}_{\bullet}\colon

\displaystyle(P^{{\cal Y}_{\bullet}}_{k}+P^{{\cal Y}_{\bullet}}_{j})({\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}})/P^{{\cal Y}_{\bullet}}_{j}

\displaystyle\longrightarrow g^{\rm PD}_{\bullet*}((P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{k}+P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{j})({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}}/P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{j}))

Hence the following morphism

(6.2.6)

\displaystyle{\rm deg}(u)^{-(j+1)}g^{{\rm PD}*}_{\bullet}\colon

\displaystyle(P_{2j+k+1}+P^{{\cal Y}_{\bullet}}_{j})({\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}})/P^{{\cal Y}_{\bullet}}_{j}

\displaystyle\longrightarrow g^{\rm PD}_{\bullet*}((P_{2j+k+1}+P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{j})({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}})/P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{j})

for
k\in{\mathbb{Z}}
and
j\in{\mathbb{N}}
is well-defined.

Lemma 6.3
.

The divisibility assumption for the morphism

(
6.2.1
)
is independent of the choices of
the affine open coverings of
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}

and
Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}\bullet}
and the choices of
the simplicial immersions of

(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet}
over
\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}
and

Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}\bullet}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal Q}_{\bullet}
over
\overline{S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}

giving the commutative diagram
(
6.0.4
)
.

Proof.

Let
(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime}_{\bullet}

and

(Y^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}\bullet},C^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}\bullet})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal Q}{}^{\prime}_{\bullet}

be other immersions into log smooth simplicial schemes over
\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}

and
\overline{S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}
, respectively, fitting another commutative diagram

\begin{CD}(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}\bullet})@>{\subset}>{}>\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\!\!\!\bullet}\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
(Y^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}\bullet},C^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}\bullet})@>{\subset}>{}>\overline{\cal Q}{}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}.\end{CD}

Then, by considering products as in the proof of (
6.1
),
we may assume that there exists the following commutative diagram

\begin{CD}(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet})@>{\subset}>{}>\overline{\cal P}{}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}@>{}>{}>\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\!\!\!\bullet}\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
(Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}\bullet},C_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}\bullet})@>{\subset}>{}>\overline{\cal Q}{}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}@>{}>{}>\overline{\cal Q}{}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}.\end{CD}

Because the question is local, we may assume that
there exists an immersion
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}

(resp.
Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal Q}
) into a log smooth scheme
over
\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}

with morphism
\overline{\cal P}\longrightarrow\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime}

(resp.
\overline{\cal Q}\longrightarrow\overline{\cal Q}{}^{\prime}
) of log smooth schemes over
\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}

(resp.
\overline{S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}
)
such that the composite morphism

X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}\longrightarrow\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime}
is also an immersion
(resp.
Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal Q}\longrightarrow\overline{\cal Q}{}^{\prime}
).
Let
\overline{\mathfrak{D}}
and
\overline{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{\prime}

be the log PD-envelopes of the immersions
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}

and
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime}

over
(\overset{\circ}{T},{\cal J},\delta)
, respectively.
We may also assume that
there exists the following commutative diagram

(6.3.1)

\begin{CD}X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}@>{\subset}>{}>\overline{\cal P}{}^{\rm ex}=({\cal X},{\cal D})@>{}>{}>\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}=({\cal X}^{\prime},{\cal D}^{\prime})\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}@>{\subset}>{}>\overline{\cal Q}{}^{\rm ex}=({\cal Y},{\cal C})@>{}>{}>\overline{\cal Q}{}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}=({\cal Y}^{\prime},{\cal C}^{\prime}).\end{CD}

Set
{\mathfrak{D}}:=\overline{\mathfrak{D}}\times_{{\mathfrak{D}}(\overline{S(T)^{\natural}})}S(T)^{\natural}

and
{\mathfrak{D}}^{\prime}:=\overline{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{\prime}\times_{{\mathfrak{D}}(\overline{S(T)^{\natural}})}S(T)^{\natural}
.
Let
(\overline{\cal E},\overline{\nabla})
and
(\overline{\cal E}{}^{\prime},\overline{\nabla}{}^{\prime})
be
an
{\cal O}_{\overline{\mathfrak{D}}}
-module with integrable connection
and an
{\cal O}_{\overline{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{\prime}}
-module
with integrable connection obtained by
E
, respectively.
Set

{\cal E}:=\overline{\cal E}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}(\overline{S(T)^{\natural}})}{\cal O}_{T}

and
{\cal E}^{\prime}:=\overline{\cal E}{}^{\prime}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\mathfrak{D}}(\overline{S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}})}{\cal O}_{T^{\prime}}
.
By the local structures of the exact immersions ((
3.5
)),
we may assume that

\overline{\cal P}{}^{\rm ex}=\overline{\cal P}{}^{\prime\rm ex}\times_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}{\mathbb{A}}_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}^{c}

for a nonnegative integer
c
.
Since
E
is crystal,

\overline{\cal E}=\overline{\cal E}{}^{\prime}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}}{\cal O}_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}\langle x_{1},\ldots,x_{c}\rangle
.
Hence
{\cal E}={\cal E}^{\prime}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{T}}{\cal O}_{T}\langle x_{1},\ldots,x_{c}\rangle
.
Because
{\cal O}_{T}\langle x_{1},\ldots,x_{c}\rangle\otimes_{{\cal O}_{T}}\Omega^{i}_{{\mathbb{A}}^{c}_{\overset{\circ}{T}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

(i\in{\mathbb{N}})
is a free
{\cal O}_{T}
-module,
the morphism
{\cal O}_{T}\longrightarrow{\cal O}_{T}\langle x_{1},\ldots,x_{c}\rangle\otimes_{{\cal O}_{T}}\Omega^{i}_{{\mathbb{A}}^{c}_{\overset{\circ}{T}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

is faithfully flat.
By (
4.1.2
) and (
4.1.3
),
we have the following formula

(6.3.2)

{\cal E}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}}}\Omega^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}/\overset{\circ}{T}}/P^{\cal X}_{j}\simeq\bigoplus_{i^{\prime}+i^{\prime\prime}=i+j+1}({\cal E}^{\prime}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}}}\Omega^{i^{\prime}}_{{\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}/\overset{\circ}{T}}/P^{{\cal X}^{\prime}}_{j})\otimes_{{\cal O}_{T}}{\cal O}_{T}\langle x_{1},\ldots,x_{c}\rangle\otimes_{{\cal O}_{T}}\Omega^{i^{\prime\prime}}_{{\mathbb{A}}^{c}_{\overset{\circ}{T}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

Because we may assume that the morphism (
6.2.1
)
for the case
\bullet=0
factors through the morphism

\displaystyle g^{{\rm PD}*}\colon

\displaystyle{\cal F}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal Q}^{\rm ex}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal Q}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}}/P^{\cal Y}_{j}\longrightarrow g^{\rm PD}_{*}({\cal E}^{\prime}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}}/P^{{\cal X}^{\prime}}_{j}),

the divisibilities in

{\cal E}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}}/P^{\cal X}_{j}

(\forall i\in{\mathbb{N}})
and

{\cal E}^{\prime}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\prime}{}^{\rm ex}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}}/P^{{\cal X}^{\prime}}_{j}

(\forall i\in{\mathbb{N}})
are equivalent.
∎

Proposition 6.7
.
