Definition 6.8
.

Let
v\colon{\cal E}\longrightarrow{\cal F}

be a morphism of

f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T})
-modules (resp.
{\cal O}_{T}
-modules).
The
D
-
twist
(:=degree twist) by
k

v(-k)\colon{\cal E}(-k,u)\longrightarrow{\cal F}(-k,u)

of
v
with respect to
u

is, by definition, the morphism

\deg(u)^{k}v\colon{\cal E}\longrightarrow{\cal F}
.
This definition is well-defined for morphisms of objects of
the derived category

{\rm D}^{+}(f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T}))
(resp.
{\rm D}^{+}({\cal O}_{T})
).

Corollary 6.9
.

The morphism

(6.9.1)

\displaystyle g^{*}\colon{\rm gr}^{P}_{k}A_{\rm zar}((Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},C_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},F)\longrightarrow Rg_{*}({\rm gr}^{P}_{k}A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E))

is equal to

(6.9.2)

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k,0\}}a^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{*}(Ru_{\overset{\circ}{Y}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{C}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}*}(F_{\overset{\circ}{Y}{}^{(2j+k)}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{C}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}

\displaystyle\varpi_{\rm crys}^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}((\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{C}_{T^{\prime}_{0}})/T^{\prime})))(-j-k,u)[-2j-k]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k,0\}}\bigoplus_{\underline{\lambda}\in\Lambda^{(k)}(\overset{\circ}{g}),}a_{\phi(\underline{\lambda})\phi(\underline{\mu})*}(Ru_{\overset{\circ}{Y}_{\phi(\underline{\lambda})}\cap\overset{\circ}{C}_{\phi(\underline{\mu})}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}*}(F_{\overset{\circ}{Y}_{\phi(\underline{\lambda})}\cap\overset{\circ}{C}_{\phi(\underline{\mu})}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi_{\phi(\underline{\lambda})\phi(\underline{\mu}),{\rm crys}}(\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}/T^{\prime})))

\displaystyle(-j-k,u)[-2j-k]

\displaystyle\overset{\sum_{\underline{\lambda}\in\Lambda^{(k)}_{X}(\overset{\circ}{g})}\overset{\circ}{g}{}^{*}_{\underline{\lambda}}(-j-k,u)}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k,0\}}\bigoplus_{{\underline{\lambda}\in\Lambda^{(2j+k^{\prime})}(\overset{\circ}{g})}}a_{\underline{\lambda}\underline{\mu}*}(Ru_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{\underline{\lambda}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}_{\underline{\mu}}/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(E_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{\underline{\lambda}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}_{\underline{\mu}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi_{\underline{\lambda}\underline{\mu},{\rm crys}}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/T)))

\displaystyle(-j-k,u)[-2j-k]

\displaystyle\longrightarrow

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k,0\}}a^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{*}(Ru_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(E_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}

\displaystyle\varpi_{\rm crys}^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/T)))(-j-k,u)[-2j-k].

Proof.

This immediately follows from (
6.7
).
∎

Corollary 6.10
.

Let
h\colon X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\longrightarrow Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}

be another morphism satisfying the condition
(
6.0.3
)
,
(6.4.4)
and

(6.4.5)
.
Assume that
\overset{\circ}{g}=\overset{\circ}{h}
.
Then

\displaystyle{\cal H}^{q}(h^{*})={\cal H}^{q}(g^{*})\colon

\displaystyle{\cal H}^{q}(P_{k}A_{\rm zar}((Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},C_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},F))

\displaystyle\longrightarrow{\cal H}^{q}(Rg_{*}(P_{k}A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E)))\quad(q\in{\mathbb{N}})

Proof.

It suffices to prove that
g^{*}=h^{*}
.
This is a local question on
\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}
.
Hence we may assume that
\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}
is quasi-compact.
It suffices to prove that

{\rm gr}_{k}^{P}(h^{*})={\rm gr}_{k}^{P}(g^{*})

(k\in{\mathbb{Z}})
.
This follows from (
6.9
).
∎

Let
f\colon X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\longrightarrow S(T)^{\natural}

be the structural morphism.
By (
6.9.1
)
we have the following spectral sequence

(6.10.1)

\displaystyle E_{1}^{-k,q+k}:=E_{1}^{-k,q+k}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}):=\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}R^{q-2j-k}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}*}

\displaystyle(E_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm crys}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}))(-j-k,u)

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))\quad(q\in{\mathbb{Z}}).

Definition 6.11
.

(1) We call the spectral sequence (
6.10.1
)
the
Poincaré spectral sequence
of

\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E)

(resp.
\epsilon^{*}_{X_{T_{0}}/T}(E)
).
If
E={\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

and if
p
is locally nilpotent on
\overset{\circ}{T}
,
then we call the spectral sequence (
6.10.1
)
the
preweight spectral sequence
of

(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}
.
If
E={\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
and
if
\overset{\circ}{T}
is a flat formal
{\mathbb{Z}}_{p}
-scheme,
then we call the spectral sequence (
6.10.1
)
the
weight spectral sequence
of

(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}
.

(2) We usually denote by
P
the induced filtration on

R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))

by the spectral sequence (
6.10.1
)
twisted by
q
by abuse of notation.
We call
P
the
Poincaré filtration
on

R^{q}f_{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))
.
If
E={\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

and if
p
is locally nilpotent on
\overset{\circ}{T}
,
then we call
P
the
preweight filtration
on

R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}})
.
If
E={\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

and if
\overset{\circ}{T}
is a flat formal
{\mathbb{Z}}_{p}
-scheme,
then we call
P
the
weight filtration
on

R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}})
.

Definition 6.12

(
Abrelative Frobenius morphism).

(1) Assume that
\overset{\circ}{S}
is of characteristic
p>0
.
Let
\overset{\circ}{F}_{S}\colon\overset{\circ}{S}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{S}

be the Frobenius endomorphism of
\overset{\circ}{S}
.
Set
S^{[p]}:=S\times_{\overset{\circ}{S},\overset{\circ}{F}_{S}}\overset{\circ}{S}
.
Then we have the following natural morphisms

\displaystyle F_{S/\overset{\circ}{S}}\colon S\longrightarrow S^{[p]}

and

\displaystyle W_{S/\overset{\circ}{S}}\colon S^{[p]}\longrightarrow S.

(The underlying morphism of the former morphism is
{\rm id}_{\overset{\circ}{S}}
.)
Let
(T,{\cal J},\delta)\longrightarrow(T^{\prime},{\cal J}^{\prime},\delta^{\prime})
be a morphism of

p
-adic formal log PD-enlargements over the morphism
S\longrightarrow S^{[p]}
.
Then we have the following natural morphisms

\displaystyle S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\longrightarrow S^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}

and

\displaystyle(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)\longrightarrow(S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},{\cal J}^{\prime},\delta^{\prime}).

We call the morphisms
S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\longrightarrow S^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}

and

(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)\longrightarrow(S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},{\cal J}^{\prime},\delta^{\prime})

the
abrelative Frobenius morphism of base log schemes

and the
abrelative Frobenius morphism of base log PD-schemes
, respectively.
(These Frobenius morphisms are essentially absolute
in the logarithmic structures: these are relative in the scheme structures;
“abrelative” is a coined word;
it means “absolute and relative” or “far from being relative”.)
In particular, when
(T^{\prime},{\cal J}^{\prime},\delta^{\prime})=(T,{\cal J},\delta)

with morphism
T_{0}\longrightarrow S
,
we have the following natural morphisms

\displaystyle S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\longrightarrow S^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}

and

\displaystyle(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)\longrightarrow(S^{[p]}(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)

by using a composite morphism

T_{0}\longrightarrow S\overset{W_{S/\overset{\circ}{S}}}{\longrightarrow}S^{[p]}
.

(2) Let the notations be as in (1).
Set
(X^{[p]},D^{[p]}):=(X,D)\times_{S}S^{[p]}

and

\displaystyle(X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})

\displaystyle:=(X^{[p]},D^{[p]})\times_{S^{[p]}}S^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}.

Then
(X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}

is an SNCL scheme with a relative SNCD over
S^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}
.
Let

F^{\rm ar}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},S^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}}\colon(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},C_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\longrightarrow(X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},C^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})

and

F^{\rm ar}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}\colon(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\longrightarrow(X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})

be the natural morphisms
over
S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\longrightarrow(S^{[p]})_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}
and

(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)\longrightarrow(S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},{\cal J}^{\prime},\delta^{\prime})
.
We call

F^{\rm ar}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},S^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}}

and

F^{\rm ar}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}

the
abrelative Frobenius morphisms

of
(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})
over
S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\longrightarrow S^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}

and
(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)\longrightarrow(S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},{\cal J}^{\prime},\delta^{\prime})
, respectively.

Assume that
{\cal O}_{T}
is
p
-torsion-free and that
{\cal J}\subset p{\cal O}_{T}
.
Let
E
and
E^{\prime}
be a flat quasi-coherent crystal of

{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
-modules and
a flat quasi-coherent crystal of

{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{[p]}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}}
-modules,
respectively.
Let

(6.12.1)

\displaystyle\Phi^{\rm ar}\colon\overset{\circ}{F}{}^{{\rm ar}*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},{\rm crys}}(E^{\prime})\longrightarrow E

be a morphism of crystals in

(\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T})_{\rm crys}
.
Since
\deg(F_{S(T)^{\natural}/S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}})=p
,
the divisibility of the morphism (
6.2.1
) holds by (
6.6
)
if
{\cal I}\subset p{\cal O}_{T}
.
We call the following induced morphism by
\Phi^{\rm ar}

(6.12.2)

\displaystyle\Phi^{\rm ar}\colon

\displaystyle(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},E^{\prime}),P^{D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}},P)

\displaystyle\longrightarrow RF^{\rm ar}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}*}((A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P))

the
abrelative Frobenius morphism
of

(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)\quad{\rm and}\quad(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},E^{\prime}),P).

When
E^{\prime}={\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{[p]}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}}
,
we set

(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}),P^{D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}}.P):=(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},E^{\prime}),P^{D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}},P).

Then we have the following
abrelative Frobenius morphism

(6.12.3)

\displaystyle\Phi^{\rm ar}\colon

\displaystyle(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}),P^{D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}},P)\longrightarrow

\displaystyle RF^{\rm ar}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}*}((A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}),P^{D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}.P))

of
(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)
and

(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}),P^{D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}},P)
.

Proposition 6.13

(
Frobenius compatibility I).

The following diagram is commutative
:

(6.13.1)

\begin{CD}A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},E^{\prime})@>{\Phi^{\rm ar}}>{}>\\
@A{\theta_{(X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}\wedge}A{\simeq}A\\
Ru_{(X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{[p]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}(E^{\prime}))@>{\Phi^{\rm ar}}>{}>\end{CD}

\begin{CD}RF^{\rm ar}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}*}(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E))\\
@A{RF^{\rm ar}_{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}/S(T)^{\natural},S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}*}(\theta_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}})\wedge}A{\simeq}A\\
RF^{\rm ar}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}*}Ru_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(E).\end{CD}

This is contravariantly functorial
for the morphism
(
6.0.2
)
satisfying
(
6.0.3
)

and for the morphism of
F
-crystals

\begin{CD}\overset{\circ}{F}{}^{{\rm ar}*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},{\rm crys}}(E^{\prime})@>{\Phi^{\rm ar}}>{}>E\\
@A{}A{}A@A{}A{}A\\
\overset{\circ}{g}{}^{*}\overset{\circ}{F}{}^{{\rm ar}*}_{(Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},C_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},S^{[p]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},{\rm crys}}(F^{\prime})@>{\overset{\circ}{g}{}^{*}(\Phi^{\rm ar})}>{}>\overset{\circ}{g}{}^{*}(F),\end{CD}

where
F

(
resp.
F^{\prime})
is
a similar quasi-coherent

{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
-module to
E

(
resp. a similar quasi-coherent

{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{Y}{}^{[p]}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
-module to
E^{\prime})
.

Proof.

This is a special case of (
6.4
).
∎

Definition 6.14

(
Absolute Frobenius endomorphism).

Let the notations and the assumptions be as in (
6.12
).
Let
F_{S}\colon S\longrightarrow S
be the Frobenius endomorphism of
S
,
that is,
\overset{\circ}{F}_{S}\colon\overset{\circ}{S}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{S}

is induced by the
p
-th power endomorphism of

and the multiplication by
p
of the log structure of

.

be

.
Assume that there exists a lift

be the absolute Frobenius endomorphism over

be a morphism of crystals in

)

) for the case

if

Then we call the induced morphism by

and

(6.14.1)

\displaystyle\Phi^{\rm abs}\colon

\displaystyle(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)\longrightarrow

\displaystyle RF^{\rm abs}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}*}((A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P))

the

of

.

,
we have the following

(6.14.2)

\Phi^{\rm abs}{}^{*}\colon(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)\longrightarrow RF^{\rm abs}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}((A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P))

of
(A_{\rm zar}(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)

with respect to

.

When

is of characteristic

, when

Corollary 6.15

(
Contravariant functoriality
of the (pre)weight spectral sequence(a generalization of the
p
-adic analogue of

[
SaT
, Corollary 2.12]
)).

Let the notations and the assumption be as above
and in
(
6.1
)
or
(
6.4
)
.
Assume that the two conditions

(6.4.4)
and
(6.4.5)
hold.
Let
\overset{\circ}{g}{}^{(l),(m)*}
be the following morphism
:

(6.15.1)

\displaystyle\overset{\circ}{g}{}^{(l),(m)*}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\underline{\lambda}\in\Lambda^{(l)}_{X}(\overset{\circ}{g}),\underline{\mu}\in\Lambda^{(m)}_{D}(\overset{\circ}{g})}\overset{\circ}{g}{}^{*}_{\underline{\lambda}\underline{\mu}}\colon R^{q}f_{\overset{\circ}{Y}{}^{(l)}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{C}{}^{(m)}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}*}(F_{\overset{\circ}{Y}{}^{(l)}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{C}{}^{(m)}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(l,m)}_{\rm crys}((\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{C}_{T^{\prime}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}))

\displaystyle\longrightarrow R^{q}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}*}(E_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(l,m)}_{\rm crys}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}})/T)).

Then there exists the following morphism of

(
pre
)
weight spectral sequences
:

(6.15.2)

\displaystyle E_{1}^{-k,q+k}=\bigoplus_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}R^{q-2j-k}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(E_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}

\displaystyle\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm crys}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}))(-j-k,u)\Longrightarrow R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))

\begin{CD}{\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad}{\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad}\\
@A{\bigoplus_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}{\rm deg}(u)^{j+k}\overset{\circ}{g}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})*}}A{}A@A{}A{g^{*}}A\\
\end{CD}

\displaystyle E_{1}^{-k,q+k}=\bigoplus_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}R^{q-2j-k}f_{\overset{\circ}{Y}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{C}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(F_{\overset{\circ}{Y}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{C}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}

\displaystyle\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm crys}((\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T^{\prime}_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{C}_{T^{\prime}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}))(-j-k,u)\Longrightarrow R^{q}f_{(Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},C_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S(T^{\prime})^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}(F)).

Proof.

This follows from (
6.4
), (
6.7
)
and from the definition of
\overset{\circ}{g}{}^{(l,m)}

(l,m\in{\mathbb{N}})
.
∎

7
Monodromy operators

Let
S
,
((T,{\cal J},\delta),z)
and
T_{0}
be as in §
3
.
In this section we recall the mornodromy operator defined in

[
HK
]
and
[
N4
]
quickly.
Let
Y
be a log smooth scheme over
S
.
Let
Y^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
be the disjoint union of the member of
an affine open covering of
Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
.
By abuse of notation, we also denote by
g

the composite morphism

g\colon Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\longrightarrow S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\longrightarrow S(T)^{\natural}
.
Let
Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}
be the Čech diagram of

Y^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
over
Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
:

Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0},n}={\rm cosk}_{0}^{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}(Y^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})_{n}

(n\in{\mathbb{N}})
.
Let
g_{\bullet}\colon Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}\longrightarrow S(T)^{\natural}

be the structural morphism.
For
U=S(T)^{\natural}
or
\overset{\circ}{T}
,
let

\epsilon_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/U}\colon((Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/U)_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/U})\longrightarrow((\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}})

be the morphism forgetting the log structures of
Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}
and
U
.
Let

Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal Q}_{\bullet}

be an immersion into a log smooth scheme over
\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}
.
Set
{\cal Q}_{\bullet}:=\overline{\cal Q}_{\bullet}\times_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}S(T)^{\natural}
.
Let
\overline{\mathfrak{E}}_{\bullet}

be the log PD-envelope of
the immersion
Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}
over

(\overset{\circ}{T},{\cal J},\delta)
.
Set
{\mathfrak{E}}_{\bullet}=\overline{\mathfrak{E}}_{\bullet}\times_{{\mathfrak{D}}(\overline{S(T)^{\natural}})}S(T)^{\natural}
.
Let
\theta_{{\cal Q}_{\bullet}}\in{\Omega}^{1}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

be the pull-back of
\theta=d\log t\in{\Omega}^{1}_{S(T)^{\natural}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
by the structural morphism

{\cal Q}_{\bullet}\longrightarrow S(T)^{\natural}
.
Let
\overline{F}
be a flat quasi-coherent crystal of

{\cal O}_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
-modules.
Let
\overline{F}{}^{\bullet}
be the crystal of

{\cal O}_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
-modules obtained by
\overline{F}
.
Let
(\overline{\cal F}{}^{\bullet},\overline{\nabla})
be the quasi-coherent

{\cal O}_{\overline{\mathfrak{E}}_{\bullet}}
-module
with integrable connection corresponding to

\overline{F}{}^{\bullet}
.
Set

({\cal F}^{\bullet},\nabla)=(\overline{\cal F}{}^{\bullet},\overline{\nabla})\otimes_{{\cal O}_{\overline{\mathfrak{E}}_{\bullet}}}{\cal O}_{{\mathfrak{E}}_{\bullet}}
.
Let

(7.0.1)

\nabla\colon{\cal F}^{\bullet}\longrightarrow{\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{1}_{{\cal Q}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

be the induced connection by
\overline{\nabla}
.
Since
{\Omega}^{1}_{{\cal Q}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}

is a quotient of

{\Omega}^{1}_{{\cal Q}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}
,
we also have the induced connection

(7.0.2)

\nabla_{/S(T)^{\natural}}\colon{\cal F}^{\bullet}\longrightarrow{\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{1}_{{\cal Q}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}

by
\nabla
.
The object

({\cal F}^{\bullet},\nabla_{/S(T)^{\natural}})

corresponds to the log crystal

F^{\bullet}:=\epsilon_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}^{*}(\overline{F}{}^{\bullet})
of
{\cal O}_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}
-modules.

In
[
N4
]
we have proved the following whose proof is not difficult:

Proposition 7.1

(
[
N4
, (1.7.22)]
).

The following sequence

(7.1.1)

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\longrightarrow{\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}[-1]\overset{\theta_{{\cal Q}_{{\bullet}}\wedge}}{\longrightarrow}{\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\longrightarrow{\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}\longrightarrow 0

is exact.

Let

(7.1.2)

{\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}\longrightarrow{\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}

be the boundary morphism of (
7.1.1
)
in the derived category

D^{+}(f^{-1}_{T}({\cal O}_{T}))
.
(We make the convention on the sign of
the boundary morphism as in
[
NS
, p. 12 (4)]
.)
By using the formula

Ru_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}*}(F^{\bullet})={\cal F}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}

(
[
Ka1
, (6.4)]
, (cf.
[
NS
, (2.2.7)]
)),
we have the following morphism

(7.1.3)

Ru_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}*}(F^{\bullet})\longrightarrow Ru_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}*}(F^{\bullet}).

Let

(7.1.4)

\pi_{{\rm crys}}\colon((Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}})\longrightarrow((Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural})_{\rm crys},{\cal O}_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}})

and

(7.1.5)

\pi_{{\rm zar}}\colon((\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T_{0}\bullet})_{\rm zar},g^{-1}_{\bullet}({\cal O}_{T}))\longrightarrow((\overset{\circ}{Y}_{T_{0}})_{\rm zar},g^{-1}({\cal O}_{T}))

be the natural morphisms of ringed topoi.
Applying
R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}
to (
7.1.3
)
and using the formula

\pi_{{\rm zar}*}\circ u_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}}=u_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}}\circ\pi_{{\rm crys}}

and using the cohomological descent,
we have the following morphism

(7.1.6)

N_{\rm zar}\colon Ru_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}*}(F)\longrightarrow Ru_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}*}(F).

In
[
N4
]
we have proved the following whose proof is not difficult:

Proposition 7.2

(
[
N4
, (1.7.26), (1.7.30)]
).

The morphism
(
7.1.6
)

is independent of the choices of an affine open covering of

Y
and a simplicial immersion

Y_{\bullet}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{{\cal Q}}_{\bullet}
over
\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}
.

Let

v\colon(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)\longrightarrow(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)
be
an endomorphism of
(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)
.
Let

(7.2.1)

\begin{CD}Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}@>{h}>{}>Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}@>{v_{0}}>{}>S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\\
@V{\bigcap}V{}V@V{}V{\bigcap}V\\
S(T)^{\natural}@>{v}>{}>S(T)^{\natural}\end{CD}

be a commutative diagram of log schemes.
The morphism (
7.1.6
)
is nothing but a morphism

(7.2.2)

N_{\rm zar}\colon Ru_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}*}(F)\longrightarrow Ru_{Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}*}(F)(-1;v)

since
v^{*}(\theta_{{\cal Q}_{\bullet}})=\deg(v)\theta_{{\cal Q}_{\bullet}}
.
In
[
N4
]
we have called the morphism (
7.2.2
) the

zariskian monodromy operator

of
Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}
.
In particular we obtain the following monodromy operator:

(7.2.3)

N_{\rm zar}\colon Ru_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(F)\longrightarrow Ru_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(F)(-1;v)

Next we express the monodromy operator (
7.2.3
) by using
the isomorphism (
5.3.1
) and the complex

A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E)
.

Let

(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})^{\bullet\bullet},P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)\longrightarrow(I^{\bullet\bullet},P)

be the cosimplicial
bifiltered Godement resolution.
Then we have a resolution

(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet}),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)\longrightarrow(s(I^{\bullet\bullet}),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P),

where
s
means the single complex
of the double complex.
Set
(A^{\bullet},P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P):=\pi_{{\rm zar}*}((I^{\bullet\bullet},P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P))
.
Then

s((A^{\bullet},P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P))=(A_{\rm zar}(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)

in
{\rm D}^{+}{\rm F}^{2}(f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T}))
.
Consider the following morphism

(7.2.4)

\displaystyle\nu_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})^{ij}:={\rm proj}\colon{\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{S}}/P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{j+1}\longrightarrow{\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+j+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{S}}/P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{j+2}.

It is easy to check that
the morphism above actually induces
a morphism of complexes
with the boundary morphisms in (
5.0.11
).
Since
(I^{\bullet\bullet},P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)
is
the bifilteredly Godement resolution of

(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})),P)
,
the morphism (
7.2.4
)
induces the morphism

\widetilde{\nu}^{ij}_{{\rm zar}}:={\rm proj}.\colon A^{ij}\longrightarrow A^{i-1,j+1}

of sheaves of
f^{-1}({\cal O}_{T})
-modules.
Set

(7.2.5)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\nu}_{{\rm zar}}:=s(\oplus_{i,j\in{\mathbb{N}}}\widetilde{\nu}^{ij}_{{\rm zar}}).

Let

(7.2.6)

\nu_{S(T)^{\natural},{\rm zar}}\colon(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)\longrightarrow(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P\langle-2\rangle)

be a morphism of bifiltered complexes induced by

\{\nu^{ij}_{S(T)^{\natural},{\rm zar}}\}_{i,j\in{\mathbb{N}}}
.
The morphism

\theta_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}\wedge\colon(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})^{ij},P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)\longrightarrow(A_{\rm zar}({\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural},{\cal E}^{\bullet})^{i,j+1},P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)

in (
5.0.11
) induces a morphism

\theta_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}\wedge\colon A^{\bullet,ij}\longrightarrow A^{\bullet,i,j+1}.

Since the following diagram

(7.2.7)

\begin{CD}A^{\bullet,ij}@>{{\rm proj}.}>{}>A^{\bullet,i-1,j+1}\\
@V{(g^{{\rm PD}*}_{\bullet})^{(ij)}}V{}V@V{}V{\deg(u)(g^{{\rm PD}*}_{\bullet})^{(i-1,j+1)}}V\\
g^{{\rm PD}*}_{\bullet*}(A^{\bullet,ij})@>{{\rm proj}.}>{}>g^{{\rm PD}*}_{\bullet*}(A^{\bullet,i-1,j+1})\end{CD}

is commutative, the morphism (
7.2.6
) is
the following morphism

(7.2.8)

\displaystyle\nu_{S(T)^{\natural},{\rm zar}}

\displaystyle\colon(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)

\displaystyle\longrightarrow(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P\langle-2\rangle)(-1,u).

Let
g
be the morphism (
6.0.2
) for the case

Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}=X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}

and
(T^{\prime},{\cal J}^{\prime},\delta^{\prime})=(T,{\cal J},\delta)
and
S^{\prime}=S

satisfying the condition (
6.0.3
):

(7.2.9)

\begin{CD}X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}@>{g^{\prime}}>{}>X^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}@>{g}>{}>X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}@>{}>{}>S_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}_{0}}\\
@V{\bigcap}V{}V@V{}V{\bigcap}V\\
S(T)^{\natural}@>{u}>{}>S(T)^{\natural},\end{CD}

where
X^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}

is another disjoint union
of the member of an affine open covering of

X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
.
Assume that
\deg(u)
is not divisible by
p
or
that
\overset{\circ}{T}
is a
p
-adic formal scheme and
that the morphism (
6.2.1
) is divisible by
p^{e_{p}(j+1)}
.

Set

(7.2.10)

B^{\bullet ij}:=A^{\bullet ij}\oplus A^{\bullet i-1,j}(-1,u)\quad(i,j\in{\mathbb{N}})

and

(7.2.11)

B^{\bullet\penalty\hskip 0.0pti\kern-0.16391pt\penalty\hskip 0.0ptj}:=A^{\bullet ij}\oplus A^{\bullet i-1,j}(-1,u)\quad(i,j\in{\mathbb{N}}).

The horizontal boundary morphism

d^{\prime}\colon B^{\bullet ij}\longrightarrow B^{\bullet i+1,j}

is, by definition, the induced morphism

d^{\prime\prime}{}^{\bullet}\colon B^{\bullet ij}\longrightarrow B^{\bullet i+1,j}

defined by the following formula:

(7.2.12)

\displaystyle d^{\prime}(\omega_{1},\omega_{2})=(\nabla\omega_{1},-\nabla\omega_{2})

and the vertical one

d^{\prime\prime}\colon B^{\bullet ij}\longrightarrow B^{\bullet i,j+1}
is the induced morphism
by a morphism
d^{\prime\prime}{}^{\bullet}\colon B^{\bullet ij}\longrightarrow B^{\bullet i,j+1}

defined by the following formula:

(7.2.13)

\displaystyle d^{\prime\prime}{}^{\bullet}(\omega_{1},\omega_{2})=(\theta_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}\wedge\omega_{1},-\theta_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}\wedge\omega_{2}+\nu_{S(T)^{\natural},{\rm zar}}(\omega_{2})).

It is easy to check that

B^{\bullet\bullet}
is
actually a double complex.
Let
B^{\bullet}
be the single complex of
B^{\bullet\bullet}
.

Let

(7.2.14)

\displaystyle\mu_{{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}}\colon\widetilde{R}u_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}(E))=

\displaystyle R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow B^{\bullet}

be a morphism
of complexes induced by the following morphisms

\displaystyle\mu^{i}_{\bullet}\colon{\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}\longrightarrow

\displaystyle~{\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}/P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{0}\oplus{\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i+1}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}/P^{{\cal X}_{\bullet}}_{0}\quad(i\in{\mathbb{N}})

defined by the following formula

\displaystyle\mu^{i}_{\bullet}(\omega):=(\omega~{\rm mod}~P_{0},\theta_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}\wedge\omega~{\rm mod}~P_{0})\quad(\omega\in{\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{i}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}}).

Then we have the following
morphism of triangles:

(7.2.15)

\begin{CD}@>{}>{}>A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E)[-1]@>{}>{}>\\
@A{(\theta_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}\wedge*)[-1]}A{}A\\
@>{}>{}>R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}\Omega^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}})[-1]@>{R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(\theta_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}\wedge)}>{}>\end{CD}

\begin{CD}B^{\bullet}@>{}>{}>A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E)@>{+1}>{}>\\
@A{\mu_{{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}}}A{}A@A{\theta_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}\wedge}A{}A\\
R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/\overset{\circ}{T}})@>{}>{}>R\pi_{{\rm zar}*}({\cal E}^{\bullet}\otimes_{{\cal O}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}}}{\Omega}^{\bullet}_{{\cal P}^{\rm ex}_{\bullet}/S(T)^{\natural}})@>{+1}>{}>.\end{CD}

This is nothing but the following diagram of triangles:

(7.2.16)

\begin{CD}@>{}>{}>A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E)[-1]@>{}>{}>\\
@A{(\theta_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}\wedge)[-1]}A{}A\\
@>{}>{}>Ru_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))[-1]@>{}>{}>\end{CD}

\begin{CD}B^{\bullet}@>{}>{}>A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E)@>{+1}>{}>\\
@A{\mu_{{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}}}A{}A@A{\theta_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}\wedge}A{}A\\
\widetilde{R}u_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}}(E))@>{}>{}>Ru_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))@>{+1}>{}>.\end{CD}

Proposition 7.3
.

The zariskian monodromy operator

(7.3.1)

\displaystyle N_{S(T)^{\natural},{\rm zar}}\colon

\displaystyle Ru_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))

\displaystyle\longrightarrow Ru_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))(-1,u)

is equal to

(7.3.2)

\displaystyle\nu_{S(T)^{\natural},{\rm zar}}\colon A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E)\longrightarrow A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E)(-1,u)

via the isomorphism
(
5.3.1
)
.

Proof.

Since
B^{\bullet}=\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(I^{\bullet\bullet})\oplus\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(I^{\bullet\bullet})[-1]

is the mapping fiber of

\nu_{S(T)^{\natural},{\rm zar}}\colon\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(I^{\bullet\bullet})\longrightarrow\pi_{{\rm zar}*}(I^{\bullet\bullet})(-1,u)

by (
7.2.13
), we obtain (
7.3
).
∎

Proposition 7.4
.

The morphism
(
7.3.2
)

is an underlying morphism of the following morphism

(7.4.1)

\displaystyle\nu_{S(T)^{\natural},{\rm zar}}\colon

\displaystyle(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)

\displaystyle\longrightarrow(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P\langle-2\rangle)(-1,u).

Proof.

This follows from the definition of
P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}
,
P
and

\nu_{S(T)^{\natural},{\rm zar}}
.
Here
P\langle-2\rangle_{k}:=P_{k-2}
.
∎

8
Bifiltered base change theorem

In this section we prove the filtered base change theorem
of
(A_{\rm zar},P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)
.

Let the notations be as in the previous section.
Assume that
\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}
is quasi-compact.
Let
f\colon X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\longrightarrow S(T)^{\natural}

be the structural morphism.

Proposition 8.1
.

Assume that
\overset{\circ}{f}\colon\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{T}

is quasi-compact and quasi-separated.
Then
Rf_{*}((A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P))

is isomorphic to a bounded filtered complex of

{\cal O}_{T}
-modules.

Proof.

By
[
BO1
, 7.6 Theorem]
,

Rf_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(E_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}})

(0\leq l,m\in{\mathbb{N}})
is bounded.
Hence

Rf_{*}((A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P))

is bounded by the spectral sequence (
6.10.1
).
∎

Theorem 8.2

(
Log base change theorem of

(A_{\rm zar},P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)
).

Let the assumptions be as in
(
8.1
)
.
Let
(T^{\prime},{\cal J}^{\prime},\delta^{\prime})
be another log PD-enlargement over
S
.
Assume that
{\cal J}^{\prime}
is quasi-coherent.
Set
T^{\prime}_{0}:=\underline{\rm Spec}^{\log}_{T^{\prime}}({\cal O}_{T^{\prime}}/{\cal J}^{\prime})
.
Let
u\colon(S(T^{\prime})^{\natural},{\cal J}^{\prime},\delta^{\prime})\longrightarrow(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)
be
a morphism of fine log PD-schemes.
Let

f^{\prime}\colon(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})=(X,D)\times_{S}S_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}\longrightarrow S(T^{\prime})^{\natural}

be the base change morphism of
f

by the morphism
S(T^{\prime})^{\natural}\longrightarrow S(T)^{\natural}
.
Let
q\colon(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})\longrightarrow(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})

be the induced morphism by
u
.
Then there exists
the following canonical filtered isomorphism

(8.2.1)

\displaystyle Lu^{*}Rf_{*}((A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P))\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle Rf^{\prime}_{*}((A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S(T^{\prime})^{\natural},\overset{\circ}{q}{}^{*}_{\rm crys}(E)),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}},P))

in
{\rm DF}(f^{\prime}{}^{-1}({\cal O}_{T^{\prime}}))
.

Proof.

Let the notations be as in §
5
.
Set

\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet,\overline{S(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}}:=\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet}\times_{\overline{S(T)^{\natural}}}\overline{S(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}
.
Let

\overline{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{\prime}_{\bullet}

be the log PD-envelope of the immersion

(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0},\bullet},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0},\bullet})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet,\overline{S(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}}

over
(\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime},{\cal J}^{\prime},\delta^{\prime})
.
Then we have the natural morphisms

\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet,\overline{S(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}}\longrightarrow\overline{\cal P}_{\bullet}

and

\overline{\mathfrak{D}}{}^{\prime}_{\bullet}\longrightarrow\overline{\mathfrak{D}}_{\bullet}
.
We also have the identity morphism

{\rm id}\colon\overset{\circ}{q}{}^{*}_{\rm crys}(E)\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{q}{}^{*}_{\rm crys}(E)
.
Obviously the morphism

q\colon X_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}\longrightarrow X_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}_{0}}
satisfies
the conditions (5.1.1.6).
Hence we have the following natural morphism

(8.2.2)

(A_{\rm zar}(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D},P)\longrightarrow Rq_{*}((A_{\rm zar}(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}/S(T^{\prime})^{\natural},E),P))

by (
6.1
).
By applying
Rf_{*}
to (
8.2.2
) and using
the adjoint property of
L
and
R
(
[
NS
, (1.2.2)]
),
we have the natural morphism (
8.2.1
).
Here we have used the boundedness in
(
8.1
) for the well-definedness of
Lu^{*}
.

The rest of the proof is the same as that of
[
N4
, (1.6.2)]
.
∎

Let
\overset{\circ}{Y}
be a smooth scheme over
\overset{\circ}{T}
.
Endow
\overset{\circ}{Y}
with the inverse image of
M_{S_{\overset{\circ}{T}}}

and let
Y
be the resulting log scheme.
Assume that
Y
has a log smooth lift
{\cal Y}
over
S(T)^{\natural}
.
Let
D_{{\cal Y}/S(T)^{\natural}}(1)
be the log PD-envelope of the immersion

{\cal Y}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}{\cal Y}\times_{S(T)^{\natural}}{\cal Y}
over
(S(T)^{\natural},{\cal J},\delta)
.
As in
[
B2
, V]
and
[
BO1
, §7]
, we have the following two corollaries
(cf.
[
NS
, (2.10.5), (2.10.7)]
) by
using (
8.2
) and
a fact that

p_{i}\colon\overset{\circ}{D}_{{\cal Y}/S(T)}(1)\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{\cal Y}

(i=1,2)

is flat (
[
Ka1
, (6.5)]
):

Corollary 8.3
.

Let
g\colon(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\longrightarrow Y
be an SNCL scheme with a relative SNCD on
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/Y
.
Let
q
be an integer.
Let
g\colon(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\longrightarrow{\cal Y}

be also the structural morphism.
Then there exists a quasi-nilpotent integrable connection

(8.3.1)

\displaystyle P_{k}R^{q}g_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/{\cal Y}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))\overset{\nabla_{k}}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle P_{k}R^{q}g_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/{\cal Y}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E)){\otimes}_{{\cal O}_{\cal Y}}{\Omega}_{{\cal Y}/S(T)^{\natural}}^{1}

making the following diagram commutative
for any two nonnegative integers
k\leq l:

(8.3.2)

\begin{CD}P_{k}R^{q}g_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/{\cal Y}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))@>{\nabla_{k}}>{}>\\
@V{\bigcap}V{}V\\
P_{l}R^{q}g_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/{\cal Y}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E))@>{\nabla_{l}}>{}>\end{CD}

\begin{CD}P_{k}R^{q}g_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/{\cal Y}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E)){\otimes}_{{\cal O}_{\cal Y}}{\Omega}_{{\cal Y}/S(T)^{\natural}}^{1}\\
@V{\bigcap}V{}V\\
P_{l}R^{q}g_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/{\cal Y}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}(E)){\otimes}_{{\cal O}_{\cal Y}}{\Omega}_{{\cal Y}/S(T)^{\natural}}^{1}.\end{CD}

Proof.

This follows from (
8.2
) as in
[
BO1
, §7]
.
∎

Corollary 8.4
.

Let the notations and the assumptions be as in
(\ref{prop:bdccd})
.
Then

Rf_{*}(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E))

and

Rf_{*}(P_{k}A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E))\quad(k\in{\mathbb{N}})

have finite tor-dimension.
Moreover, if
\overset{\circ}{T}
is noetherian and
if
\overset{\circ}{f}
is proper,
then
Rf_{*}(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E))
and

Rf_{*}(P_{k}A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E))

are perfect complexes of
{\cal O}_{T}
-modules.

In this paper we generalize
the definition of the strictly perfectness in
[
NS
, (2.10.8)]
as follows:

Definition 8.5
.

Let
A
be a noetherian commutative ring.
Let
(E^{\bullet},\{E^{(1)\bullet}_{k}\},\{E^{(2)\bullet}_{k}\})\allowbreak\in{\rm CF}^{2}(A)

be a bifiltered complex of
A
-modules. We say that

(E^{\bullet},\{E^{(1)\bullet}_{k}\},\{E^{(2)\bullet}_{k}\})

is
filteredly strictly perfect
if
(E^{\bullet},\{E^{\bullet}_{k}\})

is a bounded filtered complex of
A
-modules
and if all
E^{q}
’s and all
E_{k}^{(i)q}
’s
(i=1,2)

are finitely generated projective
A
-modules.

Using (
8.2
), K. Kato’s log base change theorem of log crystalline cohomologies
(
[
Ka1
, (6.10)]
) and
[
NS
, (2.10.10)]
,
we have the following corollary
(cf.
[
NS
, (2.10.11)]
):

Corollary 8.6
.

Let the notations
and the assumptions be as in
(\ref{coro:fctd})
.
Then the filtered complex

Rf_{*}((A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural},E),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P))

is a filtered perfect
complex of
{\cal O}_{T}
-modules, that is,
locally on
T_{\rm zar}
, filteredly quasi-isomorphic to
a filtered strictly perfect complex
(
[
NS
, (2.10.8)]
)
.

Proof.

(
8.6
)
immediately follows
from (
8.4
) and

[
NS
, (2.10.10)]
.
∎

9
Infinitesimal deformation invariance

In this section we prove the infinitesimal deformation invariance
of the pull-back of a morphism of SNCL schemes with relative SNCD’s in characteristic
p>0
on
zariskian
p
-adic bifiltered El-Zein-Steenbrink-Zucker complexes.
As in
[
BO2
]
, to prove the invariance, we use Dwork’s trick for enlarging
the radius of convergence of log
F
-isocrystals by the use of the relative Frobenius.
Precisely speaking, in our case,
we use the base change by the iteration of the
abrelative

Frobenius morphism (not usual relative Frobenius morphism) of the base scheme as in
[
N4
]
.

For a bifiltered complex
(K,P^{(1)},P^{(2)})
in the derived category of filtered complexes
(
[
NS
]
), denote
(K,P^{(1)},P^{(2)})\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}^{L}{\mathbb{Q}}
by
(K,P^{(1)},P^{(2)})_{\mathbb{Q}}

for simplicity of notation.
We omit the proofs of the results in this section.

The following is a main result in this section.

Theorem 9.1

(
Infinitesimal deformation invariance of the pull-back of a morphism on
Hirsch weight-filtered log crystalline complexes).

Let
\star
be nothing or
\prime
.
Let
n
be a positive integer.
Let
S^{\star}
be a family of log points.
Assume that
S^{\star}
is of characteristic
p>0
.
Let
F_{S^{\star}}\colon S^{\star}\longrightarrow S^{\star}
be the absolute Frobenius endomorphism.
Set
S^{{\star}[p^{n}]}:=S^{\star}\times_{\overset{\circ}{S}{}^{\star},\overset{\circ}{F}{}^{n}_{S^{\star}}}\overset{\circ}{S}{}^{\star}
.
Let
(T^{\star},{\cal J}^{\star},\delta^{\star})
be
a log
p
-adic formal PD-thickening of
S^{\star}
.
Set
T^{\star}_{0}:=T^{\star}~{\rm mod}~{\cal J}^{\star}
.
Let
f^{\star}\colon(X^{\star},D^{\star})\longrightarrow S^{\star}
be an SNCL scheme
with a relative SNCD over
S^{\star}
.
Assume that
\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{\star}_{T_{0}}
is quasi-compact.
Let
\iota^{\star}\colon T^{\star}_{0}(0)\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}T^{\star}_{0}

be an exact closed nilpotent immersion.
Set
S^{\star}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\star}_{0}}:=S^{\star}\times_{\overset{\circ}{S}{}^{\star}}\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\star}_{0}

and

S^{\star}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\star}_{0}(0)}:=S^{\star}\times_{\overset{\circ}{S}{}^{\star}}\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\star}_{0}(0)

and
(X^{\star}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\star}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}):=(X^{\star},D^{\star})\times_{S^{\star}}S^{\star}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\star}_{0}}
,

(X^{\star}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\star}_{0}}(0),D^{\star}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\star}_{0}}(0)):=(X^{\star},D^{\star})\times_{S^{\star}}S^{\star}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\star}_{0}(0)}
.
Set
(X^{\star}{}^{[p^{n}]},D^{\star}{}^{[p^{n}]}):=(X^{\star},D^{\star})\times_{S^{\star}}S^{{\star}[p^{n}]}

and
(X^{\star}{}^{[p^{n}]}_{\!\!\!\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\star}{}^{[p^{n}]}_{\!\!\!\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}):=(X^{\star}{}^{[p^{n}]},D^{\star}{}^{[p^{n}]})\times_{S^{{\star}[p^{n}]}}S^{{\star}[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
.
Note that the underlying scheme of

X^{\star}{}^{[p^{n}]}_{\!\!\!\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
is equal to that of

X^{\star}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
and that we have the log scheme

S^{{\star}[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}
by using the composite morphism

T_{0}\longrightarrow S^{\star}\longrightarrow S^{\star[p^{n}]}
,
where
the morphism
S^{\star}\longrightarrow S^{\star[p^{n}]}
is the composite morphism of
the abrelative Frobenius morphisms of
S^{[p^{m}]\star}

(0\leq m\leq n-1)
.
Let
n
be a positive integer such that the pull-back morphism

F^{n*}_{T^{\star}_{0}}\colon{\cal O}_{T^{\star}_{0}}\longrightarrow{\cal O}_{T^{\star}_{0}}

kills
{\rm Ker}({\cal O}_{T^{\star}_{0}}\longrightarrow{\cal O}_{T^{\star}_{0}(0)})
.
Let

(9.1.1)

\displaystyle g_{0}\colon(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}(0),D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}(0))\longrightarrow(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}(0),D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}(0))

be a morphism of log schemes over
S(T)^{\natural}\longrightarrow S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}

satisfying the condition
(5.1.1.6)

for

(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}(0),D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}(0))
and

(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}(0),D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}(0))
.
Then the following hold
:

(1)
There exist a canonical filtered morphism

(9.1.2)

\displaystyle g^{*}_{0}

\displaystyle:(A_{\rm zar}((X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}},P)_{\mathbb{Q}}\longrightarrow Rg_{0*}((A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)_{\mathbb{Q}})

fitting into the following commutative diagram

(9.1.4)

\begin{CD}A_{\rm zar}((X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural})_{\mathbb{Q}}@>{g^{*}_{0}}>{}>Rg_{0*}((A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}))_{\mathbb{Q}})\\
@V{\simeq}V{}V@V{}V{\simeq}V\\
Ru_{(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}@>{g^{*}_{0}}>{}>Rg_{0*}Ru_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}.\end{CD}

Here the last horizontal morphism
g_{0}^{*}
is the morphism constructed in

[
N4
, (5.3.1)]
.

(2)
Let
S^{\prime\prime}
,
(T^{\prime\prime},{\cal J}^{\prime\prime},\delta^{\prime\prime})
and
\iota^{\prime\prime}\colon T^{\prime\prime}_{0}(0)\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}T^{\prime\prime}_{0}

be analogous objects to

S^{\prime}
,
(T^{\prime},{\cal J}^{\prime},\delta^{\prime})
and
\iota^{\prime}\colon T^{\prime}_{0}(0)\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}T^{\prime}_{0}
, respectively.
Let
g^{\prime}_{0}\colon(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}(0),D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}(0))\longrightarrow(X^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime\prime}_{0}}(0),D^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime\prime}_{0}}(0))
be
a similar morphism to
g_{0}
.
Then

(9.1.5)

\displaystyle(g^{\prime}_{0}\circ g_{0})^{*}

\displaystyle=Rg^{\prime}_{0*}(g^{*}_{0})\circ g^{\prime}{}^{*}_{\!\!0}\colon Ru_{(X^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime\prime}_{0}}(0),D^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime\prime}_{0}}(0))/S^{\prime\prime}(T^{\prime\prime})^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime\prime}_{0}}(0),D^{\prime\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime\prime}_{0}}(0))/S^{\prime\prime}(T^{\prime\prime})^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}

\displaystyle\longrightarrow R(g^{\prime}_{0}\circ g_{0})_{*}Ru_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}.

(3)

(9.1.6)

\displaystyle{\rm id}^{*}_{(X_{T^{\star}_{0}}(0),D_{T^{\star}_{0}}(0))}={\rm id}_{(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural})_{\mathbb{Q}},P)}.

(4)
If
g_{0}
has a lift
g\colon(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\longrightarrow(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})
over

S_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\longrightarrow S^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}}
satisfying the condition
(5.1.1.6)
,
then
g^{*}_{0}
in
(\ref{eqn:ldefinvn})
is equal to the induced morphism by
g^{*}

for
E={\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}}

and

F={\cal O}_{(Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},C_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})/S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}
.

Corollary 9.2

(
Infinitesimal deformation invariance of
weight-bifiltered log crystalline complexes).

If
S^{\prime}=S
,
T^{\prime}=T
and
X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}(0)=X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}(0)
, then

(9.2.1)

\displaystyle(A_{\rm zar}((X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)_{\mathbb{Q}}=(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)_{\mathbb{Q}}.

Corollary 9.3

(
Infinitesimal deformation invariance of log isocrystalline
cohomologies with weight filtrations).

Let the notations be as in
(
9.2
)
.
Let
P^{D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}
and
P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}
be the induced filtrations on

R^{q}f_{(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}
and

R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}
by

(A_{\rm zar}((X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}),P^{D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}})\quad{\rm and}\quad(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}}),

respectively.
Let
P
’s be the induced filtrations on

R^{q}f_{(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}
and

R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}
by

(A_{\rm zar}((X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}),P)_{\mathbb{Q}}\quad{\rm and}\quad(A_{\rm zar}((X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}),P)_{\mathbb{Q}},

respectively.
Then

(9.3.1)

\displaystyle(R^{q}f_{(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}},P^{D^{\prime}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P)=

\displaystyle(R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}},P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}},P).

10
The
E_{2}
-degeneration of the
p
-adic weight spectral sequence

Let
{\cal V}
be a complete discrete valuation ring with perfect residue field
\kappa

of mixed characteristics.
In this section we assume that the underlying formal scheme
\overset{\circ}{S}
of
the family of log points
S
is a
p
-adic formal
{\cal V}
-scheme in the sense
of
[
O1
]
. Let
X/S
be a proper SNCL scheme. Let

(T,z)
be a flat log
p
-adic enlargement (see e.g.,
[
O2
]
or
[
N4
]
for this notion);

z
is a morphism
T_{1}\longrightarrow S
,
where
T_{1}
is an exact log subscheme of
T
defined by
p{\cal O}_{T}
.
Endow
p{\cal O}_{T}
with the canonical PD-structure.
In this section we prove the
E_{2}
-degeneration of
the
p
-adic weight spectral sequence of

X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}/S(T)^{\natural}
modulo torsion obtained by

(A_{\rm zar}(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}/S(T)^{\natural}),P)
by using
the infinitesimal deformation invariance of isocrystalline cohomologies
with weight filtrations in the previous section ((
9.3.1
)).

Theorem 10.1

(
E_{2}
-degeneration I).

Let
s
be the log point of a perfect field of characteristic
p>0
.
The spectral sequence
(\ref{eqn:espwfsp})
for the case
S=s

and
E={\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

degenerates at
E_{2}
.

Proof.

As in the proof of
[
N4
, (5.4.1)]
, we may assume that

T={\cal W}(s)
. In this case we have the absolute Frobenius endomorphism

F_{{\cal W}(s)}\colon{\cal W}(s)\longrightarrow{\cal W}(s)
.
If
\overset{\circ}{s}
is the spectrum of a finite field,
then the
E_{1}
-term

E^{-k,q+k}_{1}
of (
6.15.2
) is of pure weight of
q+k

by
[
KM
, Corollary 1. 2)]
,
[
CL
, (1.2)]
and
[
N1
, (2.2) (4)]
.
However see
[
NY
, (6.11)]
for the gap of the proof
the weak-Lefschetz conjecture
for a hypersurface of a large degree in
[
B1
]
;
I have filled the gap in
[
NY
, (6.10)]
.

The rest of the proof is the same as that of
[
N4
, (5.4.1)]
.
∎

Theorem 10.2

(
E_{2}
-degeneration II).

Let
T
be a log
p
-adic enlargement of
S/{\cal V}

with structural morphism
T_{1}\longrightarrow S
.
The spectral sequence
(\ref{eqn:espwfsp})
modulo torsion
for the case
E={\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}}

degenerates at
E_{2}
.

Proof.

Because the problem is local on
\overset{\circ}{T}
, we may assume that

\overset{\circ}{T}
is quasi-compact.
By virtue of (
10.1
) and (
9.2
),
the proof of this theorem is the same as that of
[
N4
, (5.4.3)]
.
∎

11
Log convergences of the weight filtrations

In this section we prove the log convergence of
the weight filtration on the log isocrystalline cohomology sheaf
induced by the spectral sequence (
6.15.2
) if
X/S
is proper.

Roughly speaking, we can obtain all the results in this section
by using the log base change theorem of

(A_{\rm zar},P)
((
8.2
)) and by
replacing
(A_{{\rm zar},{\mathbb{Q}}},P)
defined in this paper
with
(A_{{\rm zar},{\mathbb{Q}}},P)
in
[
N4
]
.
Here we have to note that the base change morphism
satisfies the condition (5.1.1.6)
as in the proof of (
8.2
).
For this reason, we omit or sketch the proofs of almost all the results in this section.
We use fundamental notions and results in
[
N4
, (5.2)]
.

Let
{\cal V}
be a complete discrete valuation ring of mixed characteristics

(0,p)
with perfect residue field. Let
K
be the fraction field of
{\cal V}
.
Set
B=({\rm Spf}({\cal V}),{\cal V}^{*})
.
Let
S
be a
p
-adic formal family of log points over
B

such that
\overset{\circ}{S}
is a
{\cal V}/p
-scheme.
Let
(X,D)/S
be a proper SNCL scheme with a relative SNCD.

Let
n
be a nonnegative integer.
Let
T
be an object of the category

{\cal E}^{\square}_{p,n}:={\rm Enl}^{\square}_{p}(S^{[p^{n}]}/{\cal V})

of (solid)
p
-adic enlargements of
S^{[p^{n}]}/{\cal V}

(
\square=
sld or nothing) (
[
N4
, (5.1.3)]
).
Then the hollowing out
S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural}
of
S^{[p^{n}]}(T)

is a formal family of log points.
Let
z_{i}\colon T_{i}\longrightarrow S^{[p^{n}]}

(i=0,1)
be the structural morphism.
Here
T_{1}:=\underline{\rm Spec}^{\log}_{T}({\cal O}_{T}/p)
and
T_{0}:=(T_{1})_{\rm red}
.
Set
(X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{i}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{i}}):=(X,D)\times_{S}S^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{i}}=(X^{[p^{n}]},D^{[p^{n}]})\times_{\overset{\circ}{S}}\overset{\circ}{T}_{i}
,
where
(X^{[p^{n}]},D^{[p^{n}]}):=(X,D)\times_{S}S^{[p^{n}]}
.
Let

f^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}}\colon(X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{i}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{i}})\longrightarrow S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural}

be the structural morphism.
(Note that this notation is different from the notation

f
in §
5
since we add the symbol
\overset{\circ}{T}
to
f^{[p^{n}]}
as a subscript.)
Because
S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural}
is a
p
-adic formal family of log points,

(X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}

is a proper SNCL scheme
with an exact PD-closed immersion

S^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural}
.

Assume that we are given a flat coherent crystal

E_{n}=\{E_{n}(\overset{\circ}{T})\}_{T\in{\cal E}^{\square}_{p,n}}
of

{\cal O}_{\{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{[p^{n}]}_{T_{1}}/\overset{\circ}{T}\}_{T\in{\cal E}^{\square}_{p,n}}}
-modules.
Let
T
be an object of
{\cal E}^{\square}_{p,n}
.
Then we obtain the following
p
-adic iso-zariskian filtered complex

(11.0.1)

\displaystyle(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural},E(\overset{\circ}{T})),P^{D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}},P)_{\mathbb{Q}}\in{\rm D}^{+}{\rm F}(f^{-1}_{\overset{\circ}{T}}({\cal K}_{T}))

for each
T\in{\cal E}^{\square}_{p,n}
.

Proposition 11.1

(
cf.
[
N4
, (5.2.2)]
).

Let
{\mathfrak{g}}\colon T^{\prime}\longrightarrow T
be a morphism in
{\cal E}^{\square}_{p,n}
.
Then the induced morphism

g\colon X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}}\longrightarrow X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}

by
{\mathfrak{g}}

gives us the following natural morphism

(11.1.1)

\displaystyle g^{*}\colon

\displaystyle(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural},E(\overset{\circ}{T})),P)\longrightarrow Rg_{*}((A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},E(\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime})),P))

of filtered complexes in

{\rm D}^{+}{\rm F}(f_{\overset{\circ}{T}}^{-1}({\cal K}_{T}))

fitting into the following commutative diagram
:

\begin{CD}A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural},E(\overset{\circ}{T}))@>{g^{*}}>{}>\\
@A{\simeq}A{}A\\
Ru_{(X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon_{(X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}))/S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural}}(E(\overset{\circ}{T})))\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}{\mathbb{Q}}@>{g^{*}}>{}>\end{CD}

\begin{CD}Rg_{*}((A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},E(\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime})),P))\\
@A{}A{\simeq}A\\
Rg_{*}(Ru_{(X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}(E(\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}))\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}{\mathbb{Q}})).\end{CD}

For a similar morphism
{\mathfrak{h}}\colon T^{\prime\prime}\longrightarrow T^{\prime}
to
{\mathfrak{g}}
and
a similar morphism

h\colon(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime\prime}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime\prime}_{1}})\longrightarrow(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}})
to
g
,
the following relation

\displaystyle(h\circ g)^{*}=Rh_{*}(g^{*})\circ h^{*}\colon

\displaystyle(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural},E(\overset{\circ}{T})),P)

\displaystyle\longrightarrow R(h\circ g)_{*}((A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime\prime}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime\prime}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T^{\prime\prime})^{\natural},E(\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime\prime})),P))

holds.

{\rm id}_{X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}}^{*}={\rm id}_{(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural},E(\overset{\circ}{T})),P)}.

Proof.

This immediately follows from the contravariant functoriality (
6.1
).
∎

The morphism (
11.1.1
) induces the following morphism

(11.1.2)

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{g}}^{*}\colon

\displaystyle Rf^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}*}((A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural},E(\overset{\circ}{T})),P))\longrightarrow R{\mathfrak{g}}_{*}Rf^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}*}((A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T^{\prime})^{\natural},E(\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime})),P))

of filtered complexes in
{\rm D}^{+}{\rm F}(f^{-1}({\cal K}_{T}))
.

The following is a key lemma for (
11.4
) below.

Lemma 11.2

(
cf.
[
N4
, (5.2.3)]
).

Let

be the category of filtered log
p
-adically convergent isocrystals on

.
Assume that
M_{S}
is split. Let
k
be a nonnegative integer or
\infty
.
Then there exists an object

(11.2.1)

\displaystyle(R^{q}f^{[p^{n}]}_{*}(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]},D^{[p^{n}]})/K,E)),P^{D},P)

of

such that

(11.2.2)

\displaystyle(R^{q}f^{[p^{n}]}_{*}(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]},D^{[p^{n}]})/K,E)),P^{D},P)_{\overset{\circ}{T}}=(R^{q}f^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}*}(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}({\overset{\circ}{T})},E(\overset{\circ}{T}))),P^{D},P)

for any object

in

.
In particular, there exists an object

\displaystyle(R^{q}f^{[p^{n}]}_{*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X^{[p^{n}]},D^{[p^{n}]})/K}(E_{K})),P^{D},P)

of

such that

(11.2.4)

\displaystyle(R^{q}f^{[p^{n}]}_{*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X^{[p^{n}]},D^{[p^{n}]})/K}(E_{K})),P^{D^{[p^{n}]}},P)_{\overset{\circ}{T}}

\displaystyle=(R^{q}f^{[p^{n}]}_{(X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(\overset{\circ}{T})*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(\overset{\circ}{T})}(E(\overset{\circ}{T})))_{\mathbb{Q}},P^{D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}},P)

for any object
\overset{\circ}{T}
in
{\rm Enl}_{p}(\overset{\circ}{S}/{\cal V})
.

Let
A
be a commutative ring with unit element.
Recall that we have said that a filtered
A
-module
(M,P)
is filteredly flat
if

and

(\forall k\in{\mathbb{Z}})
are flat
A
-modules
(
[

, (1.1.14)]
). As a corollary of (
11.2
), we obtain the following:

(
cf.
[

, (5.2.4)]
).

For a hollow log
p
-adic enlargement

of

the filtered sheaf

(11.3.1)

\displaystyle(R^{q}f^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}*}(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural},E(\overset{\circ}{T}))),P^{D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}},P)

is a filteredly flat
{\cal K}_{T}
-modules.
In particular, the filtered sheaf

(11.3.2)

\displaystyle(R^{q}f^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural}}E(\overset{\circ}{T})),P^{D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}}.P)

is a filteredly flat
{\cal K}_{T}
-module.

(

p
-adic convergence of the weight filtration (cf.

be a nonnegative integer or

.

(11.4.1)

\displaystyle(R^{q}f^{[p^{n}]}_{*}(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]},D^{[p^{n}]})/K,E))^{\square},P^{D^{[p^{n}]}},P)

\displaystyle(R^{q}f^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}*}(A_{\rm zar}((X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural},E(\overset{\circ}{T}))),P^{D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}},P)

of

\displaystyle(R^{q}f^{[p^{n}]}_{*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X^{[p^{n}]},D^{[p^{n}]})/K}(E_{K}))^{\natural,\square},P^{D^{[p^{n}]}},P)

of

such that

(11.4.4)

\displaystyle(R^{q}f^{[p^{n}]}_{(X^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural}*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X^{[p^{n}]}_{T_{1}},D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S^{[p^{n}]}(T)^{\natural}}(E(\overset{\circ}{T}))),P^{D^{[p^{n}]}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}},P)

for any object

of

.

Proof.

By using (

), the proof is the same as that of

, (5.2.3)]
.
∎

The following is a filtered version of

, (3.5)]
.
To consider the category

Lemma 11.5

(

[

, (5.2.6)]

\displaystyle g^{*}\colon

\displaystyle\longrightarrow(R^{q}f^{\prime}{}^{[p^{n}]}_{*}(A_{\rm zar}((Y^{[p^{n}]},C^{[p^{n}]})/K^{\prime},\overset{\circ}{g}{}^{*}(E)))^{\rm sld},P).

in

.

then this morphism is an isomorphism.

(

\displaystyle\longrightarrow R^{q}f_{(\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{1}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}_{T_{1}}^{(m))}/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(E_{n}(\overset{\circ}{T})_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(l)}_{T_{1}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(m)}_{T_{1}}/\overset{\circ}{T}})_{\mathbb{Q}}

of

.

\displaystyle\{((R^{q}f_{*}(A_{\rm zar}((X,D)/K,E_{n}))^{\square},,P^{D},P),\Phi_{n})\}_{n=0}^{\infty}

.

{\cal K}_{T}
-module.

.

) (1).
