.

(2) Contravariant functoriality

Proposition 11.10
.

(1)

Let
g
be as in
(
6.1
)
.
Let the notations and the assumption be as in

(
11.8
)
.
Then the log
p
-adically convergent isocrystals

P^{D}_{k}R^{q}f_{X/K*}(\epsilon^{*}_{X/K}(E_{K}))
and
P_{k}R^{q}f_{X/K*}(\epsilon^{*}_{X/K}(E_{K}))

are contravariantly functorial.

Proposition 11.11
.

Let the notations and the assumption be as in
(
11.8
)
.
Let
{\cal V}^{\prime}/{\cal V}
be a finite extension.
Let
S^{\prime}\longrightarrow S
be a morphism of log
p
-adic formal families of log points
over
{\rm Spf}({\cal V}^{\prime})\longrightarrow{\rm Spf}({\cal V})
.
Set
K^{\prime}:={\rm Frac}({\cal V}^{\prime})
.
Let
T^{\prime}
and
T
be log
(p
-adic
)
enlargements of
S^{\prime}
and
S
, respectively.
Let
T^{\prime}\longrightarrow T
be a morphism of log
(p
-adic
)
enlargements over

S^{\prime}\longrightarrow S
. Let
u\colon S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}\longrightarrow S(T)^{\natural}
be the induced morphism.
Let
Y
be a log scheme over
S^{\prime}

which is similar to
X
over
S
.
Let
F
be a similar
F
-isocrystal of

\{{\cal O}_{\overset{\circ}{Y}{}_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}}\}_{T^{\prime}\in{\cal E}^{\square}_{p,{\cal V}}}
-modules to
E
.
Let

\bigoplus_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}R^{q}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}/K*}(E_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}/K}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi_{\underline{\mu}}(\overset{\circ}{X}/K))

be an object of

F{\textrm{-}}{\rm Isoc}^{\square}_{\star}(S/{\cal V})

such that

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}R^{q}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}/K*}(E_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}/K}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi_{\underline{\mu}}(\overset{\circ}{X}/K))_{T}

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}R^{q}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}*}(E_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{T_{0}}/\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm crys}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T^{\prime}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}))_{\mathbb{Q}}

for any object
T
of
{\rm Enl}^{\square}_{p}(S/{\cal V})
.
Then
there exists the following spectral sequence
in
F{\textrm{-}}{\rm Isoc}^{\square}(S/{\cal V}):

(11.11.1)

\displaystyle E_{1}^{-k,q+k}=\bigoplus_{k^{\prime}\leq k}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}R^{q-2j-k}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}/K}(E_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}/K}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}

\displaystyle\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm crys}((\overset{\circ}{X}_{T_{0}}+\overset{\circ}{D}_{T^{\prime}_{0}})/\overset{\circ}{T}))(-j-k,u)

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow R^{q}f_{(X,D)/K*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X,D)/K}(E_{K})).\quad(q\in{\mathbb{Z}}).

Definition 11.12
.

We call (
11.11.1
)
the
Poincaré spectral sequence
of

R^{q}f_{(X,D)/K*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X,D)/K}(E_{K}))

in
{\rm Isoc}_{p}^{\square}(S/{\cal V})
and
F{\textrm{-}}{\rm Isoc}^{\square}(S/{\cal V})
,
respectively.

(3) Monodromy and the cup product of a line bundle

Let the notations and the assumptions be as in (
11.8
).

Proposition 11.13
.

(1)
There exists the monodromy operator

\displaystyle N_{\rm zar}\colon

\displaystyle(R^{q}f_{(X,D)/K*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X,D)/K}(E_{K})),P)\longrightarrow(R^{q}f_{(X,D)/K*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X,D)/K}(E_{K})),P\langle-2\rangle)(-1)

in
{\rm IsocF}_{p}^{\square}(S/{\cal V})
.

(2)

There exists the monodromy operator

\displaystyle N_{\rm zar}\colon

\displaystyle(R^{q}f_{(X,D)/K*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X,D)/K}(E_{K})),P)\longrightarrow(R^{q}f_{(X,D)/K*}(\epsilon^{*}_{(X,D)/K}(E_{K})),P\langle-2\rangle)(-1)

in
F{\textrm{-}}{\rm IsocF}^{\square}(S/{\cal V})
.

Theorem 11.14

(
Filtered log Berthelot-Ogus isomorphism).

Let the notations and the assumptions be as in
(
11.7
)
.
Let
T
be an object of
{\rm Enl}^{\square}(S/{\cal V})
.
Let
T_{0}\longrightarrow S
be the structural morphism.
Let
f_{0}\colon X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}}\longrightarrow T_{0}

be the structural morphism.
If there exists an SNCL lift with a relative SNCD lift

f_{1}\colon(X_{1},D_{1})\longrightarrow T_{1}
of
f_{0}
,
then there exists the following canonical
filtered isomorphism

(11.14.1)

(R^{q}f_{*}({\cal O}_{(X,D)/K})^{\natural}_{T},P^{D},P)\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}(R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}}),P^{D_{T_{1}}},P).

Proof.

This follows from the proof of
[
O1
, (3.8)]
and that of (
11.7
).
∎

12
Strict compatibility

In this section we prove the strict compatibility of the pull-back of a morphism of
proper SNCL schemes
with respect to the weight filtration.
Because the proofs of the results are the same as those of
[
N4
, (5.4.6)]
and

[
N4
, (5.4.7)]
, we omit the proofs.

Theorem 12.1

(
Strict compatibility I).

Let the notations be as in
(
10.1
)
and the proof of it.
Let
(Y,C)/s^{\prime}
be an analogous object to
(X,D)/s
.
Let
f^{\prime}\colon(Y,C)\longrightarrow s^{\prime}
be the structural morphism.
Let
h\colon s\longrightarrow s^{\prime}
be a morphism of log schemes.
Let
g\colon(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{0}})\longrightarrow(Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}},C_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{0}})

be the morphism in
(
6.0.2
)
for the case
S=s
,
S^{\prime}=s^{\prime}
,

T={\cal W}(s)
and
T^{\prime}={\cal W}(s^{\prime})

satisfying the condition
(5.1.1.6)
.
Let
{\cal W}^{\prime}
be the Witt ring of
\Gamma(s^{\prime},{\cal O}_{s^{\prime}})

and set
K^{\prime}_{0}:={\rm Frac}({\cal W}^{\prime})
.
Assume that
\overset{\circ}{s}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{s}{}^{\prime}
is finite.
Let
q
be a nonnegaitve integer.
Then the induced morphism

(12.1.1)

g^{*}\colon H^{q}_{{\rm crys}}((Y,C)/{\cal W}(s^{\prime}))\otimes_{{\cal W}^{\prime}}K^{\prime}_{0}\longrightarrow H^{q}_{{\rm crys}}((X,D)/{\cal W}(s))\otimes_{\cal W}K_{0}

is strictly compatible with the weight filtration.

Theorem 12.2

(
Strict compatibility II).

Let the notations and the assumption be as in
(
10.2
)

and
(
6.0.2
)
.
Let
(Y,C)/S^{\prime}
and
T^{\prime}
be analogous objects to
(X,D)/S

and
T
, respectively.
Let
g
be the morphism in
(
6.0.2
)
satisfying the condition
(5.1.1.6)
.
Let
q
be a nonnegative integer.
Then the induced morphism

(12.2.1)

g^{*}\colon v^{*}(R^{q}f^{\prime}_{(Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}},C_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}})/S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(Y_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}},C_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}^{\prime}_{1}})/S^{\prime}(T^{\prime})^{\natural}}))_{\mathbb{Q}}\longrightarrow R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}{}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}

is strictly compatible with the weight filtration.
Consequently the induced morphism

(12.2.2)

g^{*}\colon v^{*}(R^{q}f^{\prime}_{*}({\cal O}_{(Y,C)/K^{\prime}})^{\natural,\square})\longrightarrow R^{q}f_{*}({\cal O}_{(X,D)/K})^{\natural,\square}

in
F{\textrm{-}}{\rm Isoc}^{\square}(S/{\cal V})

is strictly compatible with the weight filtration
P
’s.

13
Log
p
-adic relative monodromy-weight conjecture

Imitating the relative monodromy filtration
in characteristic
p>0
in
[
D3
, (1.8.5)]
and
the relative monodromy filtration
over the complex number field in

[
SZ
]
and
[
E
]
,
we give the following conjecture which we call
the
log
l
–adic relative monodromy-weight conjecture
:

Conjecture 13.1

(
log
p
-adic relative monodromy-weight conjecture).

Let
q
be a nonnegative integer.
Assume that
\overset{\circ}{X}
is projective over
\overset{\circ}{S}
.
Then the filtration
P
on

R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}

is the monodromy filtration of
N
relative to

P^{\overset{\circ}{D}}
, that is,
the induced morphism

(13.1.1)

\displaystyle N^{e}\colon{\rm gr}^{P}_{q+k+e}{\rm gr}^{P^{\overset{\circ}{D}}}_{k}R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}\longrightarrow

\displaystyle{\rm gr}^{P}_{q+k-e}{\rm gr}^{P^{\overset{\circ}{D}_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}}}_{k}R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}(-e)

for
e,k\in{\mathbb{N}}

is an isomorphism.

We also recall the following conjecture which is the
p
-adic version of
the conjecture by K. Kato
(
[
Ka2
]
,
[
N2
, (2.0.9;
l
)]
):

Conjecture 13.2

(
log
p
–adic monodromy-weight conjecture).

Let
q
be a nonnegative integer.
Assume that
\overset{\circ}{X}
is projective over
\overset{\circ}{S}
.
Then the filtration
P
on

R^{q}f_{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}

is the monodromy filtration of
N
, that is,
the induced morphism

(13.2.1)

N^{e}\colon{\rm gr}^{P}_{q+e}R^{q}f_{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}\longrightarrow{\rm gr}^{P}_{q-e}R^{q}f_{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}}/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}(-e)\quad(e\in{\mathbb{N}})

is an isomorphism.

Remark 13.3
.

In
[
Ka2
]
Kato suggested that
the weight filtration and the monodromy filtration on
the first log
l
-adic cohomology of the
degeneration of a Hopf surface
(this is a proper SNCL surface) are different.
However the proof in [loc. cit.] is not complete.
A generalization of his suggestion
and the totally different and complete proof for the generalization
have been given in
[
N2
, (6.5)]
.

It is evident that (
13.1
) is a generalization of
(
13.2
). Conversely (
13.2
) implies (
13.1
):

Theorem 13.4
.

Assume that
\overset{\circ}{X}
is projective over
\overset{\circ}{S}
.
If
(
13.2
)
is true,
then there exists a monodromy filtration

M
on
R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}})

relative to
P^{\overset{\circ}{D}}
and the relative monodromy filtration

M
is equal to
P
.

Proof.

By (
7.3
) and (
7.4
),

\displaystyle N(P_{k}R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}))\subset

\displaystyle P_{k-2}R^{q}f_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}*}({\cal O}_{(X_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}},D_{\overset{\circ}{T}_{1}})/S(T)^{\natural}})_{\mathbb{Q}}))_{\mathbb{Q}}\quad(k\in{\mathbb{Z}}).

It suffices to prove that the morphism
(
13.2.1
) is an isomorphism.
By (
11.4
) we may assume that
S
is the log point

({\rm Spec}({\kappa}),{\mathbb{N}}\oplus{\kappa}^{*}\longrightarrow\kappa)
.
Consider the
E_{1}
–term of (
5.6.1
).
Then, by the assumption,

N
induces an isomorphism

N^{e}\colon{\rm gr}^{P}_{q+k+e}E_{1}^{-k,q+k}\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}{\rm gr}^{P}_{q+k-e}E_{1}^{-k,q+k}(-e)

(
E_{1}^{-k,q+k}=H^{q}_{\rm ket}(D^{(k)}_{l^{\infty}},{\mathbb{Q}}_{l}))
.
By (
10.2
)
the edge morphism
d^{-k,q+k}_{r}

(r\geq 1)
is strictly compatible with
P
.
Now, by the easy lemma below and by induction on
r
,
we see that the morphism

N^{e}\colon{\rm gr}^{P}_{q+k+e}E_{r}^{-k,q+k}\longrightarrow{\rm gr}^{P}_{q+k-e}E_{r}^{-k,q+k}(-e)

is an isomorphism for any
r\geq 1
.
∎

In
[
N6
, (11.16)]
we have proved the following:

Lemma 13.5
.

Let

\begin{CD}U@>{f}>{}>V@>{g}>{}>W\\
@V{N}V{}V@V{N}V{}V@V{N}V{}V\\
U@>{f}>{}>V@>{g}>{}>W\end{CD}

be a commutative diagram of filtered objects of
an abelian category such that
g\circ f=0
and
such that the vertical moprhisms
N
’s are nilpotent.
Let
M
’s be the monodromy filtrations on

U
,
V
and
W
.
Assume that
f
and
g
are strict with respect to
M
’s.
Then the isomorphism

N^{e}\colon{\rm gr}^{M}_{e}V\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}{\rm gr}^{M}_{-e}V

induces an isomorphism

(13.5.1)

N^{e}\colon{\rm gr}^{M}_{e}({\rm Ker}g/{\rm Im}f){\longrightarrow}{\rm gr}^{M}_{-e}({\rm Ker}g/{\rm Im}f).

Corollary 13.6
.

If
\dim\overset{\circ}{X}\leq 2
, then
(
13.2
)
is ture.

Problem 13.7
.

Let
{\cal V}
be a complete discrete valuation ring of mixed characteristics

(0,p)
with perfect residue field. Let
K
be the fraction field of
{\cal V}
.
Set
B=({\rm Spf}({\cal V}),{\cal V}^{*})
.
Let
S
be a
p
-adic formal family of log points over
B

such that
\overset{\circ}{S}
is a
{\cal V}/p
-scheme.
Let
(X,D)/S
be a proper SNCL scheme with a relative SNCD over
S
.
Assume that
\overset{\circ}{S}
is connected.
Let
q
be a nonnegative integer.
Is the rank of
P_{k}R^{q}f_{(X,D)/K*}({\cal O}_{X/K})

(k\in{\mathbb{N}})
is equal to the rank of

P_{k}R^{q}f_{*}({\mathbb{Q}}_{l})
?

Theorem 13.8
.

(
13.1
)
holds if
{\rm dim}\overset{\circ}{X}\leq 2
.

Proof.

It suffices to prove that
the conjecture (
13.2
) holds for
D^{(k)}

(k\in{\mathbb{N}})
.
By
[
N1
, (5.4.1)]
the
p
-adic weight spectral sequence for
D^{(k)}
degenerates at
E_{2}
.
The conjecture for the case
q=1
has been proved by
Kajiwara-Achinger
[
A
, Thereom 3.6]
.
Hence the conjecture for the case
q=3
holds by the classical Poincaré duality.
The conjecture for the case
q=2
holds by the proof of
[
M
, (6.2.1)]
.
∎
